srī irācēntira cōḻa vēntarkkuk kāmpōjarājaṉ kāṭciyākak kāṭṭiṉa kallu itu uṭiyār irācēntira cōḻa tēvar tiruvāy moḻintaruḷi uṭiyār tirucciṟṟam palamuṭiyār kōyilil muṉ vittatu - intak kallu tiruvetirampalattut tirukkal carattil tirumuṉpattikku mēlip pattiyilē vittatu (Ep Ind. Voll. No. 132)
eṉat tillit tirukkōyilil variyappeṟṟuḷḷa kal veṭṭāl aṟiyappaṭum.
mutaṟ kulōttuṅka cōḻaṉukkuc cakōtarikaḷ iruvar. ivarkaḷ kuntavi, maturāntaki eṉpōrāvar. ivviruvarum tilliyam palavāṇarpāl elliyaṟṟa pēraṉpiṉāl tillit tirukkōyilukkuc ciṟappuṭiya tiruppaṇikaḷ ceytuḷḷārkaḷ. ivaruḷ kuntavi eṉpār, tillic ciṟṟampalap perumāṉ taṇṇīr amutu ceytaruḷutaṟkeṉa aimpatiṉ kaḻañcu niṟiyuḷḷa poṉvaṭṭilit tillip peruṅ kōyilukku uḷamuvantu vaḻaṅkiyuḷḷār. icceyti,
vatisrī tiripuvaṉac cakkaravarttikaḷ kulōttuṅka cōḻa tēvar tiruttaṅkiyār irājarājaṉ kuntavi āḻvār āḷuṭi yārkkut taṇṇīr amutu ceytaruḷ iṭṭa (mi) ṇṭam oṉṟiṉāl kuṭiñikkal niṟimaturāntakaṉ māṭi yōṭu okkum poṉ ruya aimpatiṉ kaḻañcu u (teṉṉintiyak kalveṭṭut tokuti 5) eṉavarum kalveṭṭiṟ kuṟikkap peṟṟuḷḷami kāṇalām.
kuntaviyāḻvārākiya ivvammiyār, ki.pi. 1114-ām āṇṭil tilliyampalavāṇar tirukkōyil muḻuvatum polivuṭaṉ tikaḻap poṉṉampalattiṟku mīṇṭum poṉ vēyntuḷḷār. icceyti,
nāṉilatti muḻutāṇṭa cayataraṟku
 nāṟpattu nālā māṇṭil
 mīṉaṟikaḻ nāyiṟṟu veḷḷi peṟṟa
 vurōkaṇi nāḷ iṭapap pōtāl
 tēṉilavu poḻiṟṟilli nāyakartaṅ
 kōyilelāñ cempoṉ vēyntāḷ
 eṉavarun toḻutēttum irācarācaṉ
 kantavi pū vintiyāḷē.
(vãuhãfh ï©ofh bjhFâ 5, g¡., 105)
eṉavarum kalveṭṭuc ceyyuḷāl iṉitu viḷaṅkum.
ippāṭalil cayataraṉ eṉṟatu mutaṟkulōttuṅka cōḻaṉukku vaḻaṅkum ciṟappup peyarkaḷuḷ oṉṟākum. irācarācaṉ kuntavi eṉṟatu avaṉ taṅkiyākiya kuntavi yāri mutaṟkulēttuṅkaṉatu āṭciyil nāṟpattu nālā māṇṭil avaṉ taṅki kuntaviyārāl tillic ciṟṟampalam mīṇṭum poṉvēyap peṟṟateṉpatu icceyyu ḷāl naṉku pulaṉākiṟatu.
iṉi mutaṟkulōttuṅkaṉatu maṟṟoru taṅkiyākiya maturāntaki eṉpār ki.pi. 1116 ām āṇṭil tilliyil tirucciṟṟampalamuṭiyār tirunantavaṉattiṟkum civaṉaṭiyār tiruvamutu ceyyun tirumaṭattiṟkum nivantamāka iṟiyili nilaṅkaḷ vaḻaṅkiyuḷḷār. icceyti,
tiripuvaṉac cakkaravarttikaḷ srī kulōttuṅka cōḻa tēvaṟku yāṇṭu nāṟpattāṟāvatu rājātirāja vaḷanāṭṭut taṉiyūr perumpaṟṟap puliyūr uṭiyār tirucciṟṟampala muṭiyārkkut tirunantavaṉap puṟamākavum srī mākēvararkkut tiruvamutu ceyya maṭappuṟam nam perumāṉ tiruttaṅkiyār maturāntakiyāḻvār vācciyaṉ iravitiruc ciṟṟampalamuṭiyāṉ pēril vili koṇṭa nilam kiṭāraṅkoṇṭa cōḻap pēriḷami nāṭṭu erukkaṭṭāñ cēriyāṉa jayaṅkoṇṭa cōḻa nallūrp pāl (te.i.ka.tokuti 4 eṇ 222) eṉavarum kalveṭṭup pakutiyil kuṟikkap peṟṟuḷḷami kāṇalām.
iṅku eṭuttuk kāṭṭiya kalveṭṭup pakutikaḷāl mutaṟ kulōttuṅka cōḻaṉ taṅkimārākiya irācarācaṉ kuntavi yārum maturāntaki yāḻvārum tillip peruṅkōyilukkut tiruppaṇikaḷ pala purintuḷḷami naṉku pulaṉātal kāṇalām.
mutaṟkulōttuṅka cōḻaṉatu nāṟpattāṟām āṭci yāṇṭil toṇṭi nāṭṭuk kārikikkuḷattūr talivaṉum miḻali nāṭṭu vēḷāṇmi koṇṭavaṉumākiya kaṇṭaṉ mātavaṉ eṉpāṉ tilliyam palattiṉ vaṭakīḻtticiyil coṉṉavāṟaṟivār kōyilum civapurāṇaṅkaḷi virittuk kūṟutaṟku iṭamākiya purāṇam maṇṭapamum ataṉiyoṭṭiya tirumāḷikip pattiyum malipōṉṟu uṇarntu tōṉṟa variciyākak kaṭṭiṉāṉ. icceyti nīṭūrk kōyilil variyap peṟṟuḷḷa kalveṭṭil,
āriyavulakam aṉittiyum kuṭikkīḻ
 ākkiya kulōttuṅka cōḻaṟ
 kāṇṭoru nāṟpattāṟiṭit tilli
 yampalattē vaṭa kīḻpāl
 pōriyal matattuc coṉṉavāṟaṟivār
 kōyilum purāṇanūl virikkum
 purici māḷikiyum variciyā viḷaṅkap
 poruppiṉāṉ viruppuṟac ceytāṉ.... kaṇṭaṉ - mātavaṉē
eṉa varum pāṭalil viritturikkap peṟṟuḷḷami kāṇalām. ippāṭalil coṉṉavāṟaṟivār kōyil eṉṟatu, yārum yāvum kaḻaṟiṉavum aṟiyum āṟṟali iṟivaṉaruḷāṟ peṟṟa cēramāṉ perumāḷukku amitta tirukkōyilākum. tillip peruṅkōyiliṉ vaṭakīḻtticiyiṟ kaṇṭaṉ mātavaṉāṟ kaṭṭappaṭṭa ittirukkōyil kālap paḻamiyāl pēṇuvāriṉṟic citintu aḻintu pōyviṭṭa teṉak karuta vēṇṭiyuḷatu. tillip peruṅkōyilil kūttap perumāṉic cuṟṟiyuḷḷa mutal tiruccuṟṟu kulōttuṅka cōḻaṉ tirumāḷiki eṉavum, mūṉṟām tiruccuṟṟu, irācākkaḷ tampirāṉ tiruvīti eṉavum kalveṭṭukkaḷiṟ kuṟikkap peṟṟuḷḷaṉa. kulōttuṅka cōḻaṉ tirumāḷikiyākiya iraṇṭām tiruccuṟṟiṉ mēṟpuṟattil aminta kōpuravāyil kulōttuṅka cōḻaṉ tirumāḷikip puṟavāyil (te.i.ka.to.eṇ. 22) eṉak kalveṭṭiṟ kuṟikkap peṟṟuḷḷatu.
vācciyaṉ tirucciṟṟampala muṭiyāṉ caṅkaraṉāṉ teṉṉavaṉ piramarāyaṉ eṉpāṉ, perumpaṟṟappuliyūr vaṭa viṭāki maṇalūril tāṉ aṉupavittu varukiṟa maṇaṟ kolli kāl vēli nilattiyum cuṅkan tavirttaruḷiṉa kulōttuṅka cōḻa tēvar makaḷār ammaṅkiyāḻ vārāṉa periya nācciyārkkuc cēmamāka cammatittu aṉṉiya nāma karaṇattāl tillip peruṅkōyilukku aḷittuḷḷāṉ. (te.i.ka. tokuti. IV eṇ 226). aṭuttu.,
tirumaṇi poṟṟōṭ ṭeḻutu pattāṇṭil
 varutiṟi muṉṉē maṉṉavar cumantu
 tiṟi niṟittuc corinta cempoṟkuviyāl
 taṉkula nāyakaṉ tāṇṭavam payilum
 cempoṉampalañ cūḻ tirumāḷikiyum
 kōpuravācal kūṭacālikaḷum
 ulaku valaṅkoṇ ṭoḷiviḷaṅku nēmik
 kulavaṉāyutaya kuṉṟamoṭu niṉṟeṉap
 pacumpoṉ vēynta palivaḷar pīṭamum
 vicumpoḷi taḻippa viḷaṅku poṉ vēyntu
 irunilan taḻippa imiyavar kaḷippa
 periya tirunāḷ perumpeyar viḻāveṉum
 uyar pūraṭṭāti uttiraṭṭātiyil
 ampalam niṟinta aṟputak kūttar
 impar vāḻa eḻuntaruḷuvataṟkut
 tiruttērk kōyil cempoṉ vēyntu
 paruttiraḷ muttiṉ payilvaṭam parappi
 niṟimaṇi māḷiki neṭuntiruvītitaṉ
 tiruvaḷar peyarāṟ ceytu camittaruḷi
 pimpoṟkuḻitta parikala mutalāc
 cempoṟ kaṟpakattoṭu paricciṉṉamum
 aḷavillātaṉa voḷipeṟa amittup
 pattāmāṇṭiṟ cittirit tiṅkaḷ
 attam peṟṟa ātivārattut
 tiruvaḷar matiyiṉ tiriyōtacippakkattu
 iṉṉa palavum iṉitu camittaruḷi
eṉavarum vikkirama cōḻaṉiṉ meykkīrttip pakuti avaṉ ceyta tillit tiruppaṇikaḷit teḷivāka eṭutturikkiṉṟatu.
aḻakiya maṇi patitta poṉṉēṭṭiṟ kuṟippiṭṭu eḻutat takka taṉatu pattām āṭciyāṇṭil taṉakkuk kīḻppaṭinta maṉṉavarkaḷ cumantu vantu niṟitta tiṟip poruḷākiya poṟkuviyiṉik koṇṭu taṉ kulateyvamākiya civaperumāṉ tirunaṭam puriyum poṉṉampalattiṉic cūḻavuḷḷa cuṟṟu māḷikiyum ataṉiyaṭuttuḷḷa kōpuravāyilum malikaḷum ulakiṉic cūḻavuḷḷa cakkaravāḷa mali katiravaṉ utikkum utaya maliyuṭaṉ kūṭi niṉṟāṟ pōla poṉṉāl vēyappaṭṭa pali pīṭamum vāṉattil viḷaṅkap poṉvēyntu maṇṇulakattavar viṇṇavar makiḻat tāṉ piṟanta periya viḻā veṉṉum pūraṭṭāti, uttiraṭṭāti tirucciṟṟampalattiṉ aruḷum vāyniṟintu āṭum aṟputak kūttiṉi nikaḻttiyaruḷum kūttap perumāṉ ivvulakattavar vāḻa eḻuntaruḷit tiruvulā varataṟkuriya iyaṅkun tirukkōyilākiya tiruttēriṉic cempoṟ kūriyuṭiyatākap poṉvēyntu ataṉkaṇ parumi vāynta muttuvaṭaṅkaḷi variciyākat toṅkumpaṭi aṇi ceytu attērāṉatu ulā varutaṟku ēṟṟavāṟu tillip patiyil niṟimaṇi māḷikiyuṭiya neṭun tiruvītikaḷāka nāṉku vītikaḷiyum vikkirama cōḻaṉ tiruvīti eṉat taṉ peyarāl amittup paciyapoṉṉiṉāl uṭkuḻivuṭiya tākac ceyyap peṟṟa parikalam (uṇkalam) mutalākac cem poṉṉāṟ ceyyappaṭṭa kaṟpakat taruviṉiyum paric ciṉṉaṅkaḷiyum aḷavillātaṉavāka oḷipeṟa amittut taṉatu pattām āṭciyāṇṭil cittiri mātam atta viṇmīṉuṭaṉ kūṭiya ñāyiṟṟuk kiḻamiyil vaḷarpiṟit tirayōtacit titiyil ittakiya pala tiruppaṇikaḷit tillip peruṅkōyiliṟ ceytu niṟivēṟṟiṉāṉ eṉpatu mēṟ kuṟitta meykkīrtti kūṟum ceytiyākum.
ivvāṟu vikkirama cōḻaṉ tillippatiyiṟ ceyta tiruppaṇikaḷ yāvum ki.pi. 1128 ām āṇṭu ēpral mātam patiṉintām nāḷil niṟivēṟiṉa eṉpatu immeykkīrttiyiṟ kuṟikkappaṭṭuḷḷa kālakkuṟippiṉāl naṉku aṟiyappaṭum. tillicciṟṟampalattic cūḻavuḷḷa tiru māḷikip pattiyākiya mutaṟpirakāram ivvēntaṉāl amikkap peṟṟamiyiṉ vikkirama cōḻaṉ tirumāḷiki eṉa vaḻaṅkiyateṉpatu kalveṭṭukaḷāṟ pulaṉākiṉṟatu. (Ins. 282, 284 and 287 of 1913).
vikkirama cōḻaṉ perumpaṟṟappuliyūrākiya tilliyiṟ peruvītiyamitta ceytiyik kavic cakkaravartti oṭṭakkūttar ivaṉ makaṉ iraṇṭāṅkulōttuṅkaṉik kuṟittuppāṭiya kulōttuṅka cōḻaṉ piḷḷittamiḻil,
pāvaka niramputiru mālum malarōṉum
paranta patiṉeṇkaṇamum vantu paravattam
cēvaka nirampu tiruvīti puliyūriṟ
ceyta perumāṉ
(kulōttuṅka cōḻaṉ piḷḷittamiḻ 9 - 7)
eṉavarum toṭariṟ pārāṭṭip pōṟṟiyuḷḷami kāṇalām.
tillit tirukkōyiliṟ civakaṅkit tīrttattiṟku mēṟ pakkattē yuḷḷa nūṟṟukkāl maṇṭapam vikkirama cōḻaṉ paṇitta vaṇṇam avaṉuṭiya paṭittalivaṉākiya arumpākkiḻāṉ maṇaviṟ kūttaṉ kāliṅkarāyaṉ eṉpavaṉāṟ kaṭṭap peṟṟatākum. immaṇṭapattil paṉṉiraṇṭu tūṇkaḷil ` vikkiramacōḻaṉ tirumaṇṭapam eṉap peyar poṟikkap peṟṟiruttalāl im maṇṭapam vikkirama cōḻaṉ peyarāl avaṉatu paṭittalivaṉ kāliṅkarāyaṉāṟ kaṭṭap peṟṟateṉat terikiṟatu. immaṇṭapattik kaṭṭiyavaṉ arumpākkiḻāṉ maṉaviṟkūttaṉ kāliṅkarāyaṉ eṉpatu,
mallaṟ kulavariyāl nūṟṟakkāl maṇṭapattit
tillip pirāṉukkuc ceytamittāṉ - kollām
aḻivukaṇṭāṉ cēraṉ aḷappariya vāṟṟaṟ
kiḻivu kaṇṭāṉ toṇṭiyā rēṟu
(te.i.ka.to. IV pak. 33)
eṉavarum citamparam kalveṭṭuc ceyyuḷāl aṟiyappaṭum.
tilliyam palavāṇar mācimaka nāḷil kiḷḷiyiluḷḷa kaṭal tuṟiyil tīrttamāṭi vīṟṟiruntaruḷutaṟku maṇṭapamum, tilliyiliruntu kiḷḷik kaṭaṟkarikkuc cellutaṟkuriya peruvaḻiyum vikkirama cōḻaṉ āṭciyil avvēntaṉatu āṇi eṅkum nikaḻa avaṉ paṭittalivaṉākiya kāliṅkarāyaṉāl amikkap peṟṟaṉa. icceyti,
mācik kaṭalāṭi vīṟṟirukka maṇṭapamum
pēcaṟṟa aṟṟip peruvaḻiyum - īcaṟkut
teṉpuliyūrk kēyamittāṉ kūttaṉ ticiyaṉittum
maṉpuliyāṇi naṭakka vantu
(te.i.ka.to. IV pak. 34)
eṉavarum veṇpāvāl iṉitu pulaṉātal kāṇalām.
arumpākkiḻāṉ kāliṅkarāyaṉākiya ivaṉ mutaṟ kulōttuṅka cōḻaṉ āṭciyilum vikkirama cōḻaṉ āṭciyilum paṭit talivaṉāka viḷaṅkiyavaṉ. ivaṉ toṇṭi maṇṭalattu irupattu nāṉku kōṭṭaṅkaḷuḷ oṉṟākiya maṇaviṟ kōṭṭattiṉ taliyūrākiya maṇavil eṉṉum ūriṉaṉ. toṇṭiyar kōṉākiya ittalivaṉ mutaṟkulōttuṅkaṉatu āṭciyil paṭit talivaṉāyamarntu vēṇāṭu, maḻināṭu, pāṇṭināṭu, vaṭanāṭu mutaliyavaṟṟil nikaḻnta pōril taṉvēntaṉukku veṟṟiyi nalkip perumpukaḻ peṟṟāṉ. ivaṉatu veṟṟic ceyalkaḷi naṉkuṇarnta mutaṟkulōttuṅkaṉ ivaṉukkuk kāliṅkarāyaṉ eṉṉum paṭṭamaḷittup pārāṭṭiṉāṉ. ivaṉ aruḷākaraṉ, arumpākkiḻāṉ, poṉṉampalak kūttaṉ, naralōka vīraṉ mutaliya peyarkaḷāṟ pārāṭṭap peṟṟuḷḷāṉ. ivaṉ vikkiramacōḻaṉ āṭciyiṉ muṟpakutiyilum paṭit talivaṉāka iruntaṉaṉ eṉpatu oṭṭakkūttar pāṭiya vikkirama cōḻaṉulāvāl naṉku pulaṉākiṉṟatu.
cōḻa vēntarkku veṟṟi viḷikkum pōrttiṟam vāynta kāliṅkarāyaṉākiya ittalivaṉ tamiḻ makkaḷ uḷḷattilē civapattiyiṉi viḷikkum ciṟanta civaneṟic celvaṉākavum tikaḻntuḷḷāṉ. icceyti tillip peruṅkōyililum tiruvatiki vīraṭṭāṉattilum ivaṉ ceytuḷḷa tiruppaṇikaḷi veṇpā naṭiyiṟ pōṟṟi variyap peṟṟuḷḷa kalveṭṭukkaḷāl iṉitu pulaṉākum. tillip peruṅkōyilukku ivaṉ ceytuḷḷa tiruppaṇikaḷik kuṟitta muppattēḻu veṇpākkaḷ citamparam kalveṭṭiṟ poṟikkap peṟṟuḷḷami kāṇalām.
(1) tillic ciṟṟampalattil uḷḷa tirukkoṭuṅkip pakutiyip poṉṉāl vēyntatu, (2) tilliyiṟ poṉṉampalattic cempoṉṉāl vēyntatu, (3) tillip peruṅkōyililuḷḷa tiruñāṉacampantar tirukkōyilip poṉṉāl vēyntatu. (4) tillip peruṅkōyililuḷḷa pērampalattiṟku cepput takaṭu vēyntatu. (5) vaṭanāṭṭup pakiyaracar tiṟiyākat tanta cempoṉṉit tillik kūttap perumāṉukkup parikalamākac ceytaḷittatu. (6) kūttap perumāṉukkup poṟ paṭikkam ceytu koṭuttatu, (7) cempoṟkāḷañ ceytu koṭuttatu, kūttap perumāṉukku nīṇṭeriyum kaṟpūra viḷakku amittatu, poṉṉampalat tiṉic cūḻnta tiruccuṟṟil poṉ viḷakkamittatu, tillip perumāṉukku nāḷtōṟum pālamutu nivētikka nipantam aḷittatu, āyiram nāḻi neyyāl kūttap perumāṉ tirumañcaṉañ ceytaruḷak kaṇṭu makiḻntatu, civaperumāṉ ñāṉaṅ kuḻittaḷitta civañāṉap pālip parukiya tiruñāṉa campantap piḷḷiyār pāṭiyaruḷiya tiruppatikam mutalākavuḷḷa tēvārat tiruppatikaṅkaḷi nāḷun taṭiyiṉṟi ōtuvataṟkum palar kūṭiyiruntu kēṭṭu makiḻvataṟku uriya vaṇṇam tēvāra maṇṭapattik kaṭṭiyatu, tillip peruṅkōyilil mali pōṉṟu uyarnta nūṟṟukkāl maṇṭapattik kaṭṭiyatu tilliyampalattic cūḻat tiruccuṟṟu māḷiki amittatu, civakaṅkit tīrttattukkuk karuṅkaṟkaḷāl paṭiyamittatu, tillit tiruvīti cūḻa nalla oḷi viḷakkum avvitiyiṉ nāṟṟiciyilum  iṟivaṉ vīṟṟirukkun tirumaṇṭapamum ceytatu, tillik kūttapperumāṉ iṭappākattēyuḷḷa civakāmiyammikkut taṉik kōyilākac civakāmak kōṭṭam amittatu, ak kōṭṭattiṉic cūḻat tiruccuṟṟu māḷiki amittatu, civakāmiyaṉṉikku nāḷtōṟum tirumañcaṉañ ceytu vaḻipāṭu nikaḻtta ēṟpāṭu ceytatu, civakāmi yaṉṉikkup poṉṉāṭi cāttiyatu, ulakam īṉṟa aṉṉiyiṉ aruḷi niṉintu pōṟṟum muṟiyil akkōyilil nāḷtōṟum kuḻantikaṭkup pālum eṇṇeyum vaḻaṅka nipantam ceytatu, puliyūrc ciṟṟampalattip poṉmayamāṉa koṭikaḷāl alaṅkarittatu, tilliyil kūttappirāṉ tiruvītiyil eḻuntaruḷum pōtu naṟumpuki kamaḻa ēṟpāṭu ceytatu, attiruviḻāvil tiruñāṉacampantar mutalāṉa civacamaya āciriyarkaḷiṉ tiruvuruvamāṉa tirukkōlaṅkaḷi oḷi peruka eḻuntaruḷac ceytatu. tillip perumāṉi vaḻipaṭum aṉparkaḷ tilliyil taṅkiyiruntu uṇavu koḷḷum muṟiyil aṉṉam pālikka aṟakkaṭṭaḷi vakuttatu, mutaṟ kulōttuṅkaṉ paṭṭattaraciyākiya tiyākavalliyiṉ peyarāl nilam vāṅki annilattiṟkuriya variyi nīkkit tilli vāḻantaṇarkku vītiyil maṉi kaṭṭik koṭuttatu, tillip peruṅkōyilukkuk kaḷiṟṟu yāṉi koṭuttatu, tiruñāṉa campantar, tirunāvukkaracar, cuntaramūrtti cuvāmikaḷ ākiya mūvarum ellām valla mutalvaṉākiya civaperumāṉatu pukaḻttiṟattip pāṭiyavāṟu ammūvar aruḷiya tēvārap patikaṅkaḷ muḻuvatiyum muṉpōṟ citalarikka voṇṇātavāṟu aḷavotta ceppēṭukaḷil eḻutit tillip peruṅkōyiliṟ cēmittu vittatu, tillik kūttapperumāṉukkut tirunantavaṉam amittatu, tillip perumāṉ mācimaka nāḷil kaṭalāṭṭiṟku eḻuntaruḷun tiruviḻāvil ammutalvaṉ vīṟṟiruntaruḷak kiḷḷik kaṭaṟṟuṟiyil maṇṭapam amittatu, tillip peruṅkōyilukku āyiram pacukkaḷi aḷittatu, tilliyil puli maṭuviṉ arukē viyākkirapātar tantiyār mattiyantaṉa muṉivar vaḻipaṭṭa cuṭaliyamarntār tirukkōyilik kaṟṟaḷiyākkiyatu, tilli mūvāyiravarākiya antaṇarkaḷ celvap perukkamuṭiyarātal vēṇṭum eṉṉum nalla nōkkattuṭaṉ tilliyarukēyuḷḷa ēriyiliruntu taṇṇīr pāytaṟkaminta matakiṉik kalliṉāṟ ceytamittatu ākiya paṇikaḷ mēṟkuṟitta ceyyuṭ kalveṭṭil virit turittup pārāṭṭap peṟṟuḷḷaṉa. ivvāṟē maṇaviṟ kūttaṉ kāliṅkarāyaṉākiya ivaṉ tilliyiṟ pōṉṟē tiruvatiki tiruvīraṭṭāṉat tirukkōyililum pala tirup paṇikaḷic ceytuḷḷāṉ. appaṇikaḷ yāvum akkōyilil variyap peṟṟuḷḷa irupattintu veṇpākkaḷil eḻil peṟa viritturikkap peṟṟuḷḷami kāṇalām.
ivvāṟu cōḻaratu araciyal āṭcivaḷam peṟavum, tamiḻakattiṉ aruḷiyalāṭci muṟiyākiya civaneṟi vaḷarcci peṟavum maṇaviṟ kūttaṉ kāliṅkarāyaṉ ceytuḷḷa tiruppaṇikaḷil ciṟappuṭiyatākak kuṟikkat takuvatu tēvāra āciriyar mūvarum pāṭiya tiruppatikaṅkaḷ muḻuvatiyum ceppēṭukaḷil variyac ceytu civarācatāṉiyākat tikaḻum tillip peruṅkōyiliṟ cēmittu vitta tiruneṟit tamiḻp paṇiyēyākum. ittiruppaṇiyi viritturittup pōṟṟum muṟiyil amintatu,
muttiṟattār īcaṉ mutaṟṟiṟattip pāṭiyavā(ṟu)
 ottamitta ceppēṭṭi ṉuḷḷeḻuti - ittalattiṉ
 ellik kirivāy iciyeḻuti ṉāṉkūttaṉ
 tillicciṟ ṟampalattē ceṉṟu
(te.i.ka.tokuti IV. pakkam 34, ceyyuḷ 55)
eṉa varum veṇpāvākum.
iṟivaṉ tirukkōyilukkuc ceyyum tiruppaṇi, nāṭṭil vāḻum ēḻiyeḷiya makkaḷukkum naṟpayaṉaḷittal vēṇṭum eṉṉum perunōkkuṭiyavaṉ kāliṅkarāyaṉ eṉpatu, avaṉ tillic civakāmiyammi tirukkōyilil nāḷtōṟum iḷaṅ kuḻantikaḷukkup pālum eṇṇeyum vaḻaṅkumāṟu aṟakkaṭṭaḷi vakuttuḷḷa camutāyat toṇṭiṉāl naṉkuṇarappaṭum. icceyti.
celvi tiruntaṟaṅkaḷ teṉṉakarit tillikkē
nallamakap pāl eṇṇey nāṭōṟuñ - cellattāṉ
kaṇṭāṉ arumpiyarkōṉ kaṇṇakaṉīr ñālamellāṅ
koṇṭāṉan toṇṭiyār kōṉ
(te.i.tokuti IV pakkam 34, pāṭal 47)
eṉavarum veṇpāvil viritturikkap peṟṟuḷḷami kāṇalām.
tiruñāṉa campantap piḷḷiyār mutaliya tēvāra āciriyarkaḷ mūvarum pāṭiyaruḷiya tēvārat tiruppatikaṅkaḷit taṭiyiṉṟi ellārum iruntu kēṭṭaṟkuriya tēvarāya maṇṭapattit tilliyampala muṉṟililē kāliṅkarāyaṉ kaṭṭiṉāṉ. icceyti,
`naṭṭap perumāṉār ñāṉaṅ kuḻittaḷitta
ciṭṭap perumāṉ tiruppatiyam - muṭṭāmik
kēṭpōrkku maṇṭapattic ceytāṉtev vēntarkeṭa
vāṭpōkkun toṇṭiyarkōṉ maṉ
(te.i.ka.tokuti IV. pakkam. 34)
eṉavarum veṇpāviṟ kūṟappeṟṟuḷḷatu, tillip poṉṉampala muṉṟililē ippoḻutu kōvintarācap perumāḷ cannitiyiṉ kiḻakkup pakutiyē kāliṅkarāyaṉ kaṭṭiya tēvāra maṇṭapam irunta iṭamākum. ippakutiyil tēvāra āciriyar mūvarkkum tillik kūttap perumāṉi nōkkiya niliyil cannitiyiruntatu. iccannitiyilirunta tēvāra āciriyar mūvar tiruvuruvamum ippoḻutu poṉṉampalattic cūḻavuḷḷa mutaṟ pirakārattil paramāṉanta kūvattiṉ etirē piravar cannitiyi yoṭṭi eḻuntaruḷac ceyyap peṟṟami kāṇalām.
vikkirama cōḻaṉukkup piṉ avaṉ mintaṉ iraṇṭāṅ kulōttuṅka cōḻaṉ ki.pi. 1133 mutal 1150 vari in nāṭṭiṉic ciṟanta muṟiyil āṭci purinta peruvēntaṉā vāṉ. iv vēntaṉit tillit tirunakar ciṟappuṭittākat tirumuṭi cūṭiya kulōttuṅka cōḻa tēvar (te.i.ka. VII eṇ 780) eṉat tirumāṇik kuḻiyiluḷḷa kalveṭṭu ciṟappittup pōṟṟukiṉṟatu. eṉavē ivaṉatu āṭcik kālattil tillip peruṅkōyil tiruppaṇikaḷ pala iṉitu niṟivēṟṟappeṟṟut tillit tirunakaram mikavum ciṟappuṭiyatākat tikaḻvatāyiṟṟu eṉpatu naṉku pulaṉākum. iraṇṭāṅ kulōttuṅkaṉākiya ivvēntaṉ tillic ciṟṟampalap perumāṉpāl aḷavilāp pēraṉpuṭiyavaṉ eṉpatu, tilliyil naṭañcey kamalaṅkaḷi vaḷikkum cintippayaṉ (kulōttuṅkaṉ piḷḷit tamiḻ - 78) eṉavum, navanititūuy, ēttaṟ karuṅkaṭavuḷ elliyil āṉantak kūttik kaḷikūrak kumpiṭṭu( kulōt tuṅka cōḻaṉulā vari 74 - 76) eṉavum kūrum oṭṭak kūttar vāymoḻiyālum, `tillik kūttappirāṉ tiruvaṭit tāmari yiluḷḷa aruḷākiya tēṉip parukum vaṇṭu pōṉṟavaṉ (te.i.ka. tokuti IV eṇ 397) eṉat tiruvārūrk kalveṭṭu ivvēntaṉatu civapattit tiṟattiṉik kuṟippiṭutalālum naṉku viḷaṅkum.
ivvēntaṉ tillit tirunakariṉ nāṉku peruvītikaḷiyum vaṉappuṭiyaṉavāka aḻakupaṭuttiṉāṉ. paṟpala maṇṭapaṅkaḷik kaṭṭuvittāṉ. civaperumāṉ tirukkūttiyaṟṟiyaruḷum tirucciṟṟam palattit tammuṉṉōr ceytatu pōlavē poṉṉālum maṇikaḷālum aṇipeṟac ceytāṉ. tirucciṟṟampalattiṉ mukappākiya etiram palattiyum, uṭkōpurattiyum, tiruccuṟṟu māḷikiyiyum poṉmayamākkiṉāṉ. eḻunilik kōpuraṅkaḷik kaṭṭiṉāṉ. taṉ tanti vikkirama cōḻaṉ kālattiṟ kaṭṭappeṟṟa civakāmak kōṭṭattiṉi umātēviyār tām tōṉṟi imayamaliyiyum maṟakkumpaṭi mēlum virivuṭiyatākkiṉāṉ. civakāmiyammiyār tiruviḻā nāḷil ulā varutaṟkup poṉṉiṉum maṇiyiṉum aṇi ceyyap peṟṟa tēriṉic ceytu koṭuttāṉ. poṉṉālākiya kaṟpakat tarukkaḷi amittāṉ. ammi tirukkōyilukku etirēyuḷḷa civakaṅkit tīrttattiṉic cūḻa nāṟpuṟamum maṇṭapam amittāṉ. ivvāṟu ivvēntaṉ tillic ciṟṟampalatti virivupaṭuttum tiruppaṇikaḷic ceyyat toṭaṅkiya pōtu ki.pi. eṭṭām nūṟṟāṇṭil tillit tirumuṟṟattil nantivarmaṉāl piratiṭṭi ceyyappaṭṭa tillik kōvintarācar kōyili iṭamākap paṟṟi vāḻnta viṇavar cilar ittiruppaṇikkut taṭiyāy iṭiyūṟu pala purintaṉar. atu kaṇṭu ciṉamuṟṟa ivvēntaṉ attirumāl mūrttatti iṭam peyarttup piṉṉart tāṉ mēṟkoṇṭa tilliyampalat tiruppaṇiyiṉi iṉitu niṟivēṟṟiṉaṉ eṉpatu varalāṟu.
ivvēntaṉ tilliyil civakāmi ammi tirukkōyilit teṉticimēru eṉṉumpaṭi palarum vantu paṇintu taṅkumpaṭi uyarnta vimāṉamum maṇṭapamum uṭiyatāka virivupaṭuttiṉāṉ eṉpataṉi,
nīṭiya veṇṭici nīḻal vāyppa
 nēriya tekkiṇa mēru veṉṉap
 pīṭiki tilli vaṉattamitta
 periya perumāṉi vāḻttiṉavē.	           (takkayākapparaṇi)


eṉavarum tāḻiciyil oṭṭakkūttar kuṟittuḷḷār. tilliyam palattic cūḻntuḷḷa iraṇṭām tiruccuṟṟiṉit tirumāḷikip pattiyuṭaṉ amittavaṉ iraṇṭāṅ kulōttuṅkaṉ. tillip poṉṉampalattic cūḻntuḷḷa iraṇṭām tiruccuṟṟu, kulōttuṅka cōḻaṉ tirumāḷiki eṉa vaḻaṅkap peṟṟatu.
aruṇmoḻit tēvarākiya cēkkiḻāraṭikaḷik koṇṭu civaṉaṭiyārkaḷ aṟupattumūvar varalāṟukaḷiyum kuṟittut tirut toṇṭar purāṇamākiya varalāṟṟuk kāppiyattip pāṭumpaṭi  ceytavaṉ iraṇṭām kulōttuṅkaṉ eṉpatu ārāycciyāḷariṟ perumpālōr tuṇipākum. tilliyampalavaṉ `ulakelām eṉṟu aṭiyeṭuttuk koṭukkat tiruttoṇṭar purāṇattip pāṭiyaruḷiya cēkkiḻār nāyaṉār tammi ātarittup pōṟṟiya cōḻa maṉṉaṉip pattu iṭaṅkaḷil pārāṭṭip pōṟṟiyuḷḷār. appāṭalkaḷuḷ anapāyaṉ eṉṟa peyariyē ciṟappākak kuṟippiṭukiṉṟār.
ceṉṉi apayaṉ kulōttuṅkac cōḻaṉ
 tillittiruvelli
 poṉṉiṉ mayamākkiya vaḷavar pōrēṟu eṉṟum puvikākkum
 maṉṉar perumāṉ anapāyaṉ
(periyapurāṇam - caṇṭēcar. 8)
eṉavarum pāṭalil `tillit tiruvelli poṉṉiṉ mayamākkiya vaḷavar pērēṟu eṉac cēkkiḻār nāyaṉār tillip patiyil ivaṉukkuḷḷa perum paṟṟiṉip pulappaṭutti yuḷḷami kāṇalām.
tirunīṟṟuccōḻaṉ
tillinakar  ciṟappuṭittākat tirumuṭicūṭi iṟivaṉatu tiruvaruḷ vaṇṇamākiya tirunīṟṟiṉ oḷivaḷarat tillip peruṅkōyiliṟ palavakit tiruppaṇikaḷiyum ārvamuṭaṉ ceytu ittirukkōyilip poṉmaya mākkiyavaṉ anapāyaṉ eṉṉum ciṟappup peyaruṭiya cōḻa maṉṉātaliṉ avvēntar perumāṉic civaneṟic celvar palarum tirunīṟṟuc cōḻaṉ eṉac ciṟappittup pōṟṟuvārāyiṉar. tilliyampala vāṇarpāl vitta pēraṉpiṉ tiṟattāl tillip peruṅkōyiliṟ civattiruppaṇikaḷ purintu tilliyampala vāṇar eṭutta poṟpātattiṉ kīḻ eṉṟum piriyātamarntatiṉpuṟum teyvanili kivarap peṟṟa iccōḻar perumāṉi umāpati civācāriyār tām iyaṟṟiya kōyiṟ purāṇattil,
oṉṟiya cīr iravikulam uvantaruḷi yulakuyyat
 tuṉṟu pukaḻn tirunīṟṟuc cōḻaṉeṉa muṭicūṭi
 maṉṟiṉaṭan toḻutelli vaḷarkaṉaka mayamākki
 veṉṟipuṉi yanapāyaṉ viḷaṅkiya pūṅkaḻal pōṟṟi
(kōyil - pāyiram - 12)
eṉavarum pāṭalāl vaṇaṅkip pōṟṟiyuḷḷār. tillip perumāṉ tiruvītikku eḻuntaruḷum pōtu vināyakar, murukaṉ camayācāriyar mutaliyōr tiruvuruvaṅkaḷōṭu tirunīṟṟuc cōḻaṉākiya ivvēntar perumāṉ tiruvuruvamum eḻuntaruḷac ceyyap peṟṟatu eṉṟa ceyti tilliyulāvil iṭam peṟṟuḷḷami iṅkuc ciṟappākak kuṟippiṭat takuvatākum.
tilliyil naṭañcey kamalaṅkaḷi vaḷikkuñ cintip payaṉ eṉak kulōttuṅka cōḻaṉ piḷḷit tamiḻiluma,
ēttaṟ karuṅkaṭavuḷ elliyi lāṉantak
kūttik kaḷikūrak kumpiṭṭu
eṉak kulōttuṅka cōḻaṉulāvilum kaviccakkaravartti oṭṭakkūttar tilliyampatiyil anapāyaṉākiya ivvēn taṉukkuḷḷa īṭupāṭṭiṉip pulappaṭuttiyuḷḷami iṅku oppu nōkkat takuvatākum. ivaṉ mintaṉ iraṇṭām irācarāca cōḻaṉatu 17-ām āṭciyāṇṭil tirumaḻapāṭik kōyilil variyap peṟṟuḷḷa kalveṭṭoṉṟil jayaṅkoṇṭa cōḻa maṇṭalattu.... kuṉṟattūrc cēkkiḻāṉ mātēvaṭikaḷ rāmatēvaṉāṉa uttama cōḻap pallavarāyaṉ eṉpār tirumaḻapāṭit tirukkōyilukkut toṇṇūṟu pērāṭukaḷ utaviya ceyti kuṟikkappaṭṭuḷḷatu.
attakiya pukaḻvēḷāṇ marapiṟcēkki
ḻār kuṭiyil vanta aruṇmoḻit tēvarkkut
tattupari vaḷavaṉuntaṉ ceṅkō lōccun
talimiyaḷit tavartamakkut taṉatu pērum
uttama cōḻap pallavaṉ tāṉeṉṉum
uyarpaṭṭaṅ koṭuttiṭa āṅkavar nīrnāṭṭu
nittaṉuṟi tirunākēc curattilaṉpu
niṟitaliṉāl maṟavāta nilimi mikkār
eṉac cēkkiḻār purāṇaṅ kūṟumāṟu, kuṉṟattūriṟ cēkkiḻār kuṭiyiṟ piṟantu, iraṇṭām irācarācaṉ 17-ām āṭciyāṇṭil kōyilukku nipantamaḷitta kuṉṟattūrc cēkkiḻāṉ mātēvaṭikaḷ rāmatēvaṉāṉ uttama cōḻap pallavarāyaṉ eṉpavarē cēkkiḻār nāyaṉār eṉpatum, ivari ātarittut tiruttoṇṭar purāṇattip pāṭacceyta anapāyaṉ eṉ pavaṉ iraṇṭām irācarācaṉuṭiya tanti iraṇṭāṅ kulōttuṅka cōḻaṉ eṉpatum, cēkkiḻāruṭiya tampiyār pālaṟāvāyar eṉpavar iraṇṭām irācarācaṉatu 19-ām āṭciyāṇṭil tiruvaṟattuṟiyil nikaḻum māci, vikāci viḻākkaḷil tiruñāṉacampantap piḷḷiyār māṟaṉpāṭikku eḻuntaruḷutaṟku nilamaḷitta kuṉṟattūrc cēkkiḻār pālaṟāvāyaṉ kaḷappāḷarāyaṉ eṉpatum, ivar iraṇṭāṅ kulōttuṅkaṉatu āṭciyiṉ iṭippakuti toṭaṅki mūṉṟāṅ kulōttuṅkaṉ āṭciyiṉ muṟpakutivari vāḻntavareṉpatum aṟiñar mu. irākaviyaṅkār mutaliya ārāycciyāḷar tuṇipākum.
tilli  nakarcciṟappuṭittākat tirumuṭi cūṭiya iraṇṭām kulōttuṅka cōḻaṉ tillip peruṅkōyilil eḻunilik kōpuraṅkaḷik kaṭṭiṉāṉ, cēkkiḻāraṭikaḷ periyapurāṇam pāṭutaṟku muṉṉēyē tillip peruṅ kōyiluḷḷa eḻunilik kōpuraṅkaḷ nāṉkum kaṭṭap peṟṟiruntaṉa eṉpatu,
nīṭuvāṉpaṇiya uyarnta poṉvaripōl
 niliyeḻukōpuram	                        (periya - taṭut - 103)
eṉavum
niliyēḻkōpuram muṟiyē koṭutoḻu tuḷpukkār
                                               (periya - tirunāvuk - 194)
eṉavum
nīṭu nīṇilik kōpurattuḷ pukku
(periya. tiruñāṉa - 158)
eṉavum varum cēkkiḻār vāymoḻiyāl naṉku teḷiyappaṭum.
tilliyiṉ mēṟkuk kōpuram cuntarapāṇṭiyaṉ kōpuram eṉak kalveṭṭukkaḷiṟ kuṟikkap peṟiṉum (te.i.ka. tokuti IV eṇ 628-30) atu avaṉukkumuṉ aracāṇṭa cōḻarkaḷālē kaṭṭap peṟṟiruttal vēṇṭum eṉavum, ataṉi mēlum putuppittavaṉ mutal caṭiyavarmaṉ cuntara pāṇṭiyaṉ eṉavum, avvāṟē tilliyiṉ teṟkuk kōpurattip putuppittuc cokkacīyaṉ eṉṟu peyariṭṭavaṉ mutaṟ kōpperuñciṅkaṉ eṉavum, vaṭakkukkōpurattip putuppittavar kiruṣṇa tēvarāyar eṉavum koḷḷa vēṇṭiyuḷḷatu. innāṉku kōpuraṅkaḷiṉ amippum ivaṟṟiṉ mēluḷḷa karuṅkaṟciṟpaṅkaḷiṉ vaṭiva amippum ciṟpattiṉi vaṭitta kaṟkaḷum orē taṉmiyavāka amintirut talik kūrntu nōkkuṅkāl innāṉku kōpuraṅkaḷum cōḻa maṉṉarkaḷāl amikkap peṟṟuc cēkkiḻār kālattilēyē ciṟantu viḷaṅkiṉa eṉpatu iṉitu pulaṉākum.
cuntarapāṇṭiyaṉ tirunili yeḻu kōpuraccaṉṉatiyil cokkaccīyaṉ kuṟaḷil kīḻppakkattuk kīḻimaṭatāṉamākat tirunōkkaḻakiyāṉ tirumaṭameṉṉum pērāl ceyvitta maṭattukku maṭacēṣamāka nāyakarum nācciyārum eḻuntaruḷum nāḷ.... etirilicōḻaṉ tirunantavaṉattukku (te.i.ka.to.IV eṇ 624)
eṉavum
srīkiruṣṇatēvamakārāyaṉ taṉmamāka 
simhāttiṟi poṭṭuṉūṟku eḻuntaruḷi jayatampam nāṭṭi tirumpi poṉṉam palattukku eḻuntaruḷi poṉṉampala nātaṉiyum cēvittu, vaṭakkuk kōpuram kaṭṭuvitta cēli	                             (eṇ 623)
eṉavum varum kalveṭṭukkaḷ cuntarapāṇṭiyaṉ kaṭṭiyatu mēlik kōpuram eṉpataṉiyum vaṭakkuk kōpuram kiruṣṇa tēvarāyarāl kaṭṭappeṟṟa teṉpataṉiyum kuṟittuḷḷami kāṇalām.
irājākkaḷ tampirāṉ tirumāḷiki mēlit tirumāḷikiyil niliyeḻukōpurat tiruvācal teṉpakkattu eḻuntaruḷiyiruntu pūcikoṇṭaruḷukiṟa kulōttuṅka cōḻa vināyakar eṉap pāṇṭiyar kalveṭṭiṟ kuṟikkappaṭṭa talavināyakar mēlik kōpurattuṭaṉ iṇintuḷḷa tirumēṉiyātalāl apperumāṉ eḻuntaruḷiyuḷḷa mēlik kōpuram kulōttuṅka cōḻaṉāl mutaṉmutaṟ kaṭṭappeṟṟa teṉpatu uyttuṇarappaṭum.
ki.pi. 1178 mutal 1218 vari tamiḻakatti āṭci purinta cōḻa maṉṉaṉ mūṉṟāṅkulōttuṅkaṉ āvāṉ. ivaṉ tilliyil ciṟṟam palattiṟku muṉṉuḷḷatum ippoḻutu kaṉakacapiyeṉa vaḻaṅkappeṟuvatum ākiya etirampalattip poṉṉiṉāl vēyntāṉ. kūttap perumāṉukkuc cittirit tiṅkaḷil tiruviḻā nikaḻa ēṟpāṭu ceytāṉ. civakāmiyammi tirukkōyiliṉ vimāṉamākiya kōpurattip poṉṉāl vēyntāṉ. ivaṉ tilliyiṟ ceyta ittiruppaṇikaḷ ettariyum toḻum iṟivaṟku etirampalam cempoṉ vēyntu eṉavarum ivaṉuṭiya meykkīrttiyiṟ kuṟikkap peṟṟuḷḷami kāṇalām. iṅku etirampalam eṉṟatu cēkkiḻār kūṟum pērampalamē eṉavum etirampalam poṉ vēynta mūṉṟāṅ kulōttuṅkaṉē tirut toṇṭarpurāṇam kūṟum pērampalam poṉvēynta anapāyaṉ eṉavum, cēkkiḻari ātarittut tirut toṇṭar purāṇam pāṭuvittavaṉ immūṉṟāṅ kulōttuṅkaṉē eṉavum kūṟuvar ārāycciyaṟiñar catācivappaṇṭārattār.
capāpatiyiṉ muṉṉuḷḷa mukamaṇṭapattiyum mali makaḷ (civakāmiyammi) kōyiliṉ kōpurattiyum cuṟṟiyuḷḷa pirakāra māḷikikaḷiyum avvūrp perumāṉiṭattē iṭi yaṟāta pakti koṇṭa ivvaracaṉ poṉmayamāka viḷaṅkum paṭi nirmāṇittāṉ eṉat tiripuvaṉa vīrēccurat tirukkōyilil variyappaṭṭuḷḷa vaṭamoḻik kalveṭṭum, ivvēntaṉ tilliyiṟ ceyta tiruppaṇikaḷik kūṟiyuḷḷami kāṇalām. ikkalveṭṭil mukamaṇṭapam eṉṟatu, tillic ciṟṟampalavar tirumuṉ koṭimarattiṉ teṉticiyil amintuḷḷa nirutta capi yeṉak koḷḷutal ēṟpuṭiyatākum. iccapiyāṉatu kutirikaḷ pūṭṭiya tēriṉ amippiṉiyuṭiya tāyiruttaliṉ itu tērmaṇṭapam eṉavum vaḻaṅkap peṟum. (caṅkaṟpa nirākaraṇam). immaṇṭapattiṉ tūṇkaḷyāvum ivvēntaṉāl tañci māvaṭṭam tiripuvaṉattil niṟuvap peṟṟa tiripuvaṉa vīrēccurattiluḷḷa tūṇkaḷiṉ amippiṉiyuṭiyaṉavāka iruttalālum ivaṉatu vaḻipaṭu mūrttamākat tiripuvaṉattiluḷḷa carapamūrttiyiṉ tirumēṉi iccapiyilum iruttalālum immaṇṭapattiṉ vaṭapuṟattu aṭippaṭṭiyalil kūttap perumāṉi nōkkik kumpiṭum niliyil ivaṉatu uruvap paṭivam nērē amikkap peṟṟiruttalālum naṉku viḷaṅkum. ciṟiya aḷavil iccapiyil uḷḷa ivvēntaṉatu karuṅkaṟ paṭivamum tiripuvaṉa vīrēccura vimāṉattil uḷḷa ivaṉatu cutippaṭivamum oṉṟāyiruttalāl inniruttacapiyik kaṭṭiyavaṉ tiripuvaṉa vīrēccurat tirukkōyili niṟuviya mūṉṟāṅ kulōttuṅka cōḻaṉē eṉpatu naṉku teḷiyappaṭum.
tilliyil tirumāḷikip pattiyuṭaṉ kūṭiya mūṉṟām pirākāram irājākkaḷ tampirāṉ eṉṉum ciṟappup peyariṉi yuṭiya mūṉṟāṅ kulōttuṅkaṉāl amikkap peṟṟatākum. atupaṟṟiyē itu irācākkaḷ tampirāṉ tirumāḷikiyeṉa kalveṭṭil vaḻaṅkap peṟukiṉṟatu, cērapāṇṭiya maṇṭalamākiya pāṇṭi nāṭṭi ivvēntaṉ kippaṟṟiya niliyil annāṭṭil tiṟiyākap peṟṟa pacumpoṉṉum viḷi nilaṅ kaḷum ākiyavaṟṟip perumpaṟṟap puliyūr tilliyampalattilē āṭalpuriyum kūttap perumāṉuṭaṉ iruntu am mutalvaṉatu aruḷnaṭaṅ kaṇṭaruḷum civakāmiyammikkum tiruvārūrp perumāṉukkum tiripuvaṉa vīrēccurattu iṟivaṉukkum maturit tiruvālavāy iṟivaṉukkum koṭuttu makiḻntāṉ. icceyti,
ciṟi koṇṭa puṉal viyic cēra pāṇṭiya maṇṭalattu
iṟi koṇṭa pacum poṉṉum iṟiyiliyu meyiṟpuliyūr
āṭum ampalavāṇar kūṭi vāynta tirunaṭaṅ kaṇṭaruḷum
pāṭakakkāṟ piṅkiḷikkum pimpoṉ matiḷ tiruvārūr
vāṉavaṟkum tiripuvaṉa vīrīccura aruntavaṟkum
tēṉviricaṭit tiruvālavāyc ceḻucuṭarkkuṅ koṭuttaruḷi
eṉavarum ivvēntaṉatu meykkīrttiyāl aṟiyappaṭum.
ivvēntaṉatu muppattāṟāvatu āṭciyāṇṭil tillic ciṟṟampalattil tiru aṇukkaṉ tiruvāyili oṭṭi aminta kaṉaka capiyākiya etirampalattiṉ aṭippīṭamākiya kuṟaṭu ivvēntaṉāl poṟṟakaṭu pōrttap peṟṟatu. poṟṟakaṭu pōrttuvataṟkumuṉ akkuṟaṭṭil variyap peṟṟirunta kalveṭṭu paṭiyeṭukkap peṟṟu iraṇṭām pirakārattiṉi yoṭṭiya vāyilākiya kulōttuṅka cōḻaṉ tirumāḷikip puṟavāyil vaṭapakkattiṟ poṟikkappaṭṭatu. mutaṟkulōttuṅka cōḻaṉatu nāṟpattāṟāvatu āṇṭil avvēntaṉuṭiya taṅkiyār maturāntaki āḻvār tirucciṟṟampala muṭiyārkkut tirunantavaṉamākavum, srī mākēvararkkut tiruvamutu ceyya maṭappuṟamākavum nilamaḷitta ceytiyiṉik kūṟuvatu ikkal veṭṭākum.
tillip peruṅkōyiliṉ mūṉṟām pirakārattil irācākkaḷ tampirāṉ tirumāḷiki eṉat taṉpeyarāl amitta mūṉṟāṅ kulōttuṅka cōḻaṉ attiruccuṟṟiṉ mēlip pirakārattil taṉ muṉṉōr niṟuviya civakāmiyammi tirukkōyilukkut tiruppaṇi ceytatōṭu ataṉiyaṭuttuḷḷa murukap perumāṉ tirukkōyilākiya pāṇṭiya nāyakat tirukkōyiliyum niruttacapiyiṉip pōṉṟu periya tērmaṇṭapamāka amittuḷḷāṉ eṉak karuta vēṇṭi yuḷḷatu. tillik kōyilil aṇṭamuṟa nimirntāṭum kūttap perumāṉukku amikkappeṟṟuḷḷa niruttacapiyilum aṟumukap perumāṉ vaḷḷi teyvayāṉiyuṭaṉ amarntaruḷiya pāṇṭiyanāyakattilum amintuḷḷa tūṇkaḷ mūṉṟāṅ kulōttuṅkaṉāl niṟuvappeṟṟuḷḷa tiripuvaṉa vīrēccurattil uḷḷa tūṇkaḷi yotta amippuṭiyaṉavākavum, im maṇṭapaṅkaḷiluḷḷa ciṟpa amippukaḷ oru kālattaṉavākavum kāṇappaṭuvatālum ivi tamiḻakatti nāṟpatu āṇṭukaḷ nalampeṟa āṭcipurinta mūṉṟāṅkulōttuṅkaṉāl amikkap peṟṟaṉavē eṉak karuta vēṇṭiyuḷḷatu. ivvēntaṉ pāṇṭiyarkaḷi veṉṟa pāṇṭi maṇṭalattiṟkuc cōḻa pāṇṭiya maṇṭalam eṉavum, maturimānakarkku muṭittali koṇṭa cōḻapuram eṉavum, maturiyaraṇmaṉiyiluḷḷa kolumaṇṭapattiṟkuc cēra pāṇṭiyar tampirāṉ eṉavum taṉ peyarkaḷi vaḻaṅkiyuḷḷāṉ, avvāṟē ittillip patiyil pāṇṭiyar tampirāṉākiya mūṉṟāṅ kulōttuṅkaṉāṟ kaṭṭap peṟṟa murukaṉ kōyilum pāṇṭiya nāyakam eṉa vaḻaṅkap peṟuvatāyiṟṟu eṉat terikiṟatu. tillit tirukkōyiliṟ cēkkiḻār perumāṉ amarntu tiruttoṇṭar purāṇatti iyaṟṟutaṟkum, urivirittaṟkum iṭamāka viḷaṅkiyatu. āyirak kāl maṇṭapamākum. itu mūṉṟāṅ kulōttuṅkaṉākiya ivvēntaṉāṟ kaṭṭap peṟṟiruttal vēṇṭum eṉpatu ārāycciyaṟiñar catācivap paṇṭārattār tuṇipākum.
mutal kulōttuṅka cōḻaṉatu nāṟpattāṟām āṭci yāṇṭil miḻalināṭṭu vēḷāṇmi koṇṭa kaṇṭaṉ mātavaṉ eṉpāṉ tilliyampalattiṉ vaṭakīḻtticiyil purāṇa nūl virikkum puricimaḷikiyiṉi amittāṉ eṉṟa ceyti muṉṉar kūṟappaṭṭatu. appurāṇa maṇṭapattiṉi āyirakkāl maṇṭapamāka virivupaṭuttiyavaṉ mūṉṟāṅ kulōttuṅka cōḻaṉ eṉak karututal poruttamuṭiyatākum. āyirakkāl maṇṭapam kaṭṭum vaḻakkam ivaṉ kālattil tāṉ tōṉṟiyiruttal vēṇṭum eṉpar ārāycciyāḷar. tiruvārūriluḷḷa āyirakkāl maṇṭapam tēvāciriyaṉ eṉa vaḻaṅkap peṟukiṉṟatu. im maṇṭapam mūṉṟām kulōttuṅkaṉāl kaṭṭappaṭṭateṉpatu, tiripuvaṉa vīrēccurattiluḷḷa vaṭa moḻikkalveṭṭāl aṟiyappaṭum. ivvēntaṉatu paṭittalivaṉākiya kaṇṭar cūriyaṉ campuvarāyaṉ eṉpāṉ tiruvakkariyiluḷḷa civaṉ kōyilil cūriyaṉ tirukkōpuramum kaṇṭar cūriyaṉ eṉṟa āyirakkāl maṇṭapamum ākiyavaṟṟik kaṭṭiyuḷḷāṉ. eṉavē tilliyiluḷḷa āyirakkāl maṇṭapamum ivvēntaṉ kālattilēyē kaṭṭap peṟṟiruttal vēṇṭum.ivvāyirakkāl maṇṭapattilēyē cēkkiḻār nāyaṉār tām pāṭiya tiruttoṇṭar purāṇattiṟku uri virittaruḷiṉār eṉpatu umāpati civācāriyār iyaṟṟiya cēkkiḻār purāṇattāl aṟiyappaṭum.
2. pāṇṭiyar
ki.pi. 1251-71 muṭicūṭiya mutal caṭiyavarmaṉ cuntara pāṇṭiyaṉ eṉpāṉ palavāṭukaḷi veṉṟamiyāl makā rācāti rācaparamēcuvaraṉ, em maṇṭalamuṅ koṇṭaruḷiyavaṉ ellān taliyāṉa perumāḷ eṉap pala paṭṭaṅkaḷip puṉintu koṇṭāṉ. ivvēntar perumāṉ civarkaḷāl kōyil eṉap pōṟṟap peṟum tillip peruṅkōyilukkum, viṇavarkaḷāl kōyil eṉap pōṟṟappeṟum tiruvaraṅkam periya kōyilukkum pala tiruppaṇikaḷip purintuḷḷāṉ. ivaṉ tilliyiṟ kūttap perumāṉi vaṇaṅkip palatulāpāra tāṉaṅkaḷ ceytāṉ.  tilliyam palattip poṉ vēyntāṉ. tillit  tirukkōyiliṉ mēlik kōpuram `cuntara pāṇṭiyaṉ tirunili eḻukōpuram eṉṉum peyarāl (te.i.ka. tokuti 4-624) kalveṭṭukkaḷiṟ kuṟikkap peṟṟuḷḷatu. eṉavē ikkōpurattip paḻutu pārttup putuvaṉ tāṉ tulāpāram pukka poṉṉik koṇṭu tillik kōyilukkup poṉ vēyntuḷḷāṉ, itupaṟṟiyē ivvēntaṉ kōyil poṉ vēyntaperumāḷ eṉap pōṟṟap peṟṟaṉaṉ. icceyti,
vāḻka kōyil poṉvēynta makipati
 vāḻka centamiḻ māli terintavaṉ
 vāḻka maṇṭalam yāviyum koṇṭavaṉ
 vāḻka cuntara maṉṉavaṉ teṉṉaṉē.	(tokuti IV 455)
eṉat tiruppuṭkuḻi tirumāl kōyilil variyap peṟṟuḷḷa kalveṭṭup pāṭalāṟ pulaṉākum. ippāṭaliṟ kōyil eṉṟatu tillip peruṅkōyiliyum, tiruvaraṅkam periya kōyiliyum, iṅṅaṉam tilliyilum tiruvaraṅkattilumuḷḷa tirukkōyilkaḷip poṉvēyntami paṟṟik kōyil poṉ vēynta perumāṉ eṉa ivaṉ pōṟṟap peṟṟaṉaṉ eṉat terikiṟatu, (kalveṭṭu, āṇṭaṟikki 1936 -37)
ciṉavarik kimpuri veṇpiṟik kōṭṭikal veṅkaṭuṅkaṭ
ciṉamata veṅkaric cuntarat teṉṉavaṉ tilli maṉṟil
vaṉacat tiruvuṭaṉ ceñcoṟṟiruvi maṇantatokkum
kaṉakattuliyuṭaṉ muttuttuliyiṟ kalantatuvē.  (tokuti IV 620)
eṉa varum pāṭal ivaṉ tilliyil tulāpāram pukka ceytiyi yuṇarttum, ivaṉatu patiṉōrām āṭciyāṇṭil nantavaṉattukkuriya vari nīkkappaṭṭatu. cuntarapāṇṭiyaṉ tirut tōppu, cuntara pāṇṭiyaṉ teṟkut tiruvīti ivaṟṟiṉ vaḷarccik kākavum kuṭikaḷ vāḻvukkākavum nilam aḷit tuḷḷāṉ (S.I.I. 546-18) tirumūlaṭṭāṉamuṭiyārkkut tiruvuruttiram attiyayaṉam paṇṇa nilam aḷittuḷḷāṉ (A.R. Vol. VI 631). ivaṉatu āṭcik kālattil 1253 mutal 1268 vari cōḻanāṭu naṭunāṭu toṇṭi nāṭukaḷukku aracap piratinitiyāyiruntu āṇṭavaṉ. caṭiyavarmaṉ vīrapāṇṭiyaṉ āvāṉ. ivaṉ pallava aracaṉākiya iraṇṭāṅ kōpperuñciṅ kaṉiṭam tiṟi koṇṭu tilliyil vīrāpi ṭēkamum vicayāpiṭēkamum ceytu koṇṭāṉ icceyti.
tiṅkaḷ aravamuñ cemumalart tāruṭaṉ
poṅkupuṉaṟ ceñcaṭiyōṉ poṟpuliyūr vīṟṟiruntu
kāṭavaṉ tiṟiyiṭak kaṇṭiṉitiruntu
vīrāpiṣēkam vicāyapiṣēkamum paṇṇiyaruḷiya
srī vīrapāṇṭiya tēvarkku yāṇṭu, (putukkōṭṭik kalveṭṭut tokuti 370-372) eṉavarum ivaṉatu meyk kīrttit toṭarāl iṉitu pulaṉākum.
3. pallavar
cimmavarmaṉ mutaliya muṟkālap pallavarkaḷiṉ kalveṭṭukkaḷ tilliyiṟ kiṭikkavilli. cōḻamaṉṉarkaḷukkup paṭit talivarkaḷāyiruntu piṉṉar taṉiyaracu naṭattiya piṟkālap pallavarkaḷiṉ tiruppaṇikaḷē ikkōyiṟ kalveṭṭukkaḷil kāṇap peṟukiṉṟaṉa.
kāṭavarāya maṉṉarkaḷil mikavum ciṟappuṭaṉ viḷaṅkiyavarkaḷ cāṭūm perumāḷāṉa mutalām kōpperuñciṅkaṉum avaṉ makaṉ vāḷvalla perumāḷāṉa iraṇṭāvatu kōp peruñciṅkaṉum āvar. iruvarum cakalapuvaṉac cakkaravartti, avaṉiyāḷap piṟantāṉ eṉṟa paṭṭaṅkaḷip peṟṟiruntaṉar. mutalāvatu kōpperuñciṅkaṉ cōḻarkula iḷavaraciyit tirumaṇam purintavaṉ. ataṉāl maṇavāḷap perumāḷ eṉap pōṟṟap peṟṟāṉ.
mutalāvatu kōpperuñciṅkaṉ taṉ āṭciyiṉ mūṉṟāvatu, nāṉkāvatu āṇṭukaḷil tillik kōyiliṉ pāl ney tēvikaḷukkāka āyirattu eṇpattāṟu pacukkaḷi, riṣapa, cūla muttiriyuṭaṉ aḷittāṉ (S.I.I.Vol. VII No. 54). itu ivaṉ makaṉiṉ mūṉṟām āṇṭuk kalveṭṭilum kuṟippiṭappaṭṭuḷḷatu. ki.pi. 1237-il ivaṉ tillik kōyiliṉ teṟkuk kōpuram kaṭṭuvataṟkākat toṇṭi maṇṭalattu āṟṟūril munnūṟṟu oṉṟēmukkāl vēli nila varuvāyiyum piṟavarikaḷ cila vaṟṟiliruntu kiṭikkum poruḷiyum aḷittuḷḷāṉ. (S.I.I.Vol.No.51) icceytiyit tillik kōyililum, āṟṟūr muttīvarar kōyililum, kāñci ēkampamuṭiyār kōyililum kallil cetukki vittāṉ. ivaṉāl kaṭṭappaṭṭa teṟkuk kōpuram. `cokkaccīyaṉ tirunili eḻukōpurat tiruvācal eṉṟa peyaruṭaṉ viḷaṅkiyatu. cokkaccīyaṉ eṉpatu mutalāvatu kōpperuñ ciṅkaṉuṭiya virutup peyarākum. ivaṉ taṉatu 15-āvatu āṭciyāṇṭil tillik kōyiluk kāka vēcālip pāṭippaṟṟum pūvāliyil `cokkac cīyaṉ kamuku tirunantavaṉam eṉat taṉ peyarāl oru nantavaṉam amittāṉ. (S.I.I.Vol.VIII No.55) iv vaṟakkaṭṭaḷiyi ivaṉ makaṉuṭiya mūṉṟāvatu āṇṭuk kalveṭṭu oṉṟu kuṟippiṭuvatum kavaṉikkattakkatu (S.I.I.Vol.VIII No.53)
vāḷvalla perumāḷāṉa iraṇṭāvatu kōp peruñciṅkaṉ tillik kōyilil aḷavilā īṭupāṭuṭiyavaṉ. ivaṉuṭiya āṟṟūrk kalveṭṭu ivaṉik `kaṉaka capāpati capā carvakārya carvakāla nirvākaṉ  (S.I.I.Vol.XII No.120)  eṉṟum, tiripurāntakak kalveṭṭu `kaṉakacapāpati nāta caraṇāravinta matukaramāṉavaṉ  (S.I.I.Vol.XII No.247) eṉṟum kuṟikkiṉṟaṉa. ivaṉatu mūṉṟāvatu āṭciyāṇṭil citam parattiṉ mēl piṭākiyāṉa vikkirama ciṅkapurattu mēṟpuṟattil tillik kōyiliṉ tēvikkāṉa pāṅkaḷ kāykaḷ mutaliyavikaḷip peṟa `āḷiyār tiruttōppu eṉṉum tiruttōppu amikkappaṭṭatu. (S.I.I.Vol.VIII No.53) atil paṇipurivōrukkuc cokkac cīyaṉ tirunantavaṉak kuṭikaḷukku aḷikkappaṭṭa ūtiyattip pōṉṟu aḷikka ikkāṭavaṉ ēṟpāṭu ceytāṉ. atēyāṇṭil ivaṉ tillik kōyilukku nāṉūṟṟuppattup pacukkaḷi viṭṭāṉ. (S.I.I.Vol.VIII No.54) itik kūṟum kalveṭṭu ivaṉ tanti viṭṭa pacukkaḷiyum kuṟippiṭukiṟatu. ki.pi. 1251-il ivaṉ kaṭavāccēri eṉṟu taṟpoḻutu aḻikkappaṭum tilli nāyaka nallūril cāliyar (necavāḷar) cilarik kuṭiyamartti avarkaḷ tirukkāmak kōṭṭamuṭiya nācciyārkkuc cāttum paricaṭṭatti aḷikka ēṟpāṭu ceytatuṭaṉ tillināyaka nallūrukkut `tiruvampalap perumāḷ puram eṉṟa putiya peyariyum cūṭṭiṉāṉ. (S.I.I.Vol.XII No.154).  aṭutta āṇṭil ivaṉ tillik kōyilukkuriya mūṉṟu tirunantavaṉaṅkaḷiṉ parāmarippiṟkākac cila nilaṅkaḷi aḷittāṉ (16Id No 154) ivaṉ kālattiltāṉ tillik kāḷi kōyil kaṟṟaḷiyāka eṭukkap paṭṭatu. ivaṉatu 8-ām āṇṭuk kalveṭṭu akkōyil tiruppaṇi naṭipeṟṟu vantatit teḷivākak kuṟippiṭu kiṉṟatu. (S.I.I.Vol.VIII No.717). ivaṉatu pattāvatu āṭci yāṇṭil attiruppaṇi muṭivuṟṟiruttal vēṇṭum. (S.I.I.Vol.XII No.159). itaṟku muṉṉart tillikkāḷi `tillivaṉamuṭiya paramēvari eṉavum, piṉṉar piṭāriyār `tirucciṟṟampala mākāḷi eṉavum aḻikkappaṭṭatik kalveṭṭukkaḷiliruntu aṟikiṉṟōm. ikkāḷi kōyil taṟpoḻutu paḷḷip paṭi eṉṟaḻikkap paṭum vikkirama cōḻa nallūriṉ ellikkuṭpaṭṭirukkiṉṟatu. tillik kōyiliṉ kiḻakkuk kōpuram ivaṉāl kaṭṭappaṭṭati ivaṉuṭiya tiripurāntakak kalveṭṭiliruntu aṟikiṟōm. (S.I.I.Vol.XII No.247). nāṉku tikkilumirunta vēntarkaḷiveṉṟu, avar celvattik kavarntu, tulāpāram naṭatti, appoṉṉāl mērumaliyip pōṉṟa kiḻakkuk kōpurattit taṉ peyarāl ikkāṭavaṉ eṭuppittāṉ. ikkōpurattiṉ nāṉku pakkaṅkaḷiyum ivaṉ oḷimayamāka nirmāṇittuk kumpāpiṣēkattic ceytāṉ. ittiruppaṇi ki.pi. 1262-ām āṇṭiṟkuḷ naṭipeṟṟiruttal vēṇṭum. mēṟkūṟiya tiripurāntakak kalveṭṭil teṟkuk kōpurattip paṟṟiya kuṟippu etuvum illātatu kavaṉikkattakkatu. orē peyaril viḷaṅkiya tantiyum makaṉum muṟiyē teṟkuk kōpurattiyum kiḻakkuk kōpurattiyum kaṭṭiṉārkaḷ eṉṟa uṇmiyi maṟuppataṟkilli. iruvarum tillik kōyilukkum, tirukkaḷāñ ceṭiyuṭiyār kōyilukkum taṉippaṭṭōr palar aḷitta nilaṅkaḷukku varivilakkuc ceytuḷḷārkaḷ.
mutalāvatu kōpperuñ ciṅkaṉum avaṉ makaṉum nāṭṭiyak kaliyil mikunta īṭupāṭu koṇṭavarkaḷ. iruvarum `paratam valla perumāḷ eṉṟa virutup peyarik koṇṭiruntaṉar (S.I.I.Vol.XII No.69). iraṇṭāvatu kōpperuñciṅkaṉi avaṉuṭiya āṟṟūrk kalveṭṭu paratamallaṉ eṉṟum (S.I.I.Vol.XIII No.120). tiripurāntakak kalveṭṭu `cākitya ratṉākaraṉ (Ibid No. 247) eṉṟum aḻikkiṉṟaṉa. tillik kōyiliṉ teṟku kiḻakkuk kōpuraṅkaḷiṉ tiruvācal uṭpuṟac cuvaril paratanāṭṭiya muttirikaḷ cetukkappaṭṭiruppatu mēṟkūṟiya kalveṭṭuc cāṉṟiṉi uṟutippaṭuttukiṉṟatu.
4. cēramaṉṉar
cakam 1498 (ki.pi. 1576) -il cēramāṉ perumāḷ kuṭiyil tōṉṟiya kocci irāmavarma makārājā āṉantat tāṇṭava mākēcurarkaḷukkum, antaṇarkaḷukkum, paricārakarkaḷukkum nāḷtōṟum 33 taḷikiyaḷikka nipantam aḷittuḷḷār.
5. vicayanakara maṉṉar
makātēvarāyar II
ivar ki.pi. 1428-il tēvattāṉa nirvāka ūḻalkaḷit tiruttiṉār. atikavari vacūlippiṉāl kuṭikaḷ kirāmaṅkaḷi viṭṭu ōṭip pōyiṉar. ataṉāl varumāṉam kuṟintamiyāl citamparam kōyil pūci taṭippaṭṭatu. ōṭippōṉa kuṭikaḷi mīṇṭum aḻittuk kirāmaṅkaḷi vaḷappaṭuttik kōyil nirvākattic ceppañ ceytār. (376 - 1913)
timmarāyar :
vīrappiratāpa timmarāyarākiya ivar cakam 1425 (ki.pi. 1503) il cuvāmi ampāḷukku iraṭṭi māli cāttap perumpaṟṟap puliyūrkku mēṟkēyuḷḷa kārikkuṭi kirāmatti aḷittuḷḷār.
kiruṣṇatēvarāyar :
cakam 1432 (ki.pi. 1510) il aḻakiya ciṟṟampala muṭiyārkku makāpūci nikaḻavum aṭiyārkaḷukku amutu vaḻaṅkavum ēṟpāṭu ceytār. cakam 1433 (ki.pi. 1511) il citamparanātapuram eṉṟa kirāmattik kōyilukkuc carvamāṉiyamāka aḷittār. cakam 1431 (ki.pi. 1509) il cimmahāttiri poṭṭāṉūrukkuc ceṉṟu veṉṟu vaṭakkuk kōpurattik kaṭṭiṉār. (S.I.I. of 13) S.I.I. Vol.IV No.622) 
accutatēvarāyar : 
ivar tirumāl mūrttatti mīṇṭum tillik kōyilil piratiṭṭi ceytu vikāṉaca vitippaṭi pūci nikaḻa 5000 poṉ koṭuttuḷḷār.
civa, viṇava vēṟṟumiyillā ivar cakam 1451 (ki.pi. 1529) - il kūttap perumāṉ tērviḻāviṟkāka 6 kirāmaṅkaḷiyum, māṉiyamākak koṭuttuḷḷār. (Oriental Research Vol. 12 P. 169,178)
vēṅkaṭa tēvarāyar :
cakam 1500 (ki.pi. 1578) civakāma cuntariyammikkut tiruvaṉantal pūcikkākak kirāmaṅkaḷi aḷittār. cakam 1506 (ki.pi. 1613) - il 300 poṉ koṭuttu ēḻikaḷukku nāḷtōṟum 20 kaṭṭiccōṟu aḷikkum paṭi ēṟpāṭu ceytār. 5000 kalam nel varuvāyuḷḷa kirāmattiyaḷittu nāḷtōṟum tuṟavikaḷukku 30 kaṭṭic cōṟu aḷikka ēṟpāṭu ceytār. (S.I.I.346. 347-13)rf« 1586 (ki.pi. 1664)-il citamparattil irunta namacivāya uṭiyār mēṟpārviyil irupatu kaṭṭiccōṟu koṭukka nipantam aḷittuḷḷār. iṅkuk kuṟitta namacivāya uṭiyār eṉpavar kurunamacivāyarāka iruttal kūṭumō eṉṟu aiyuṟuvar cilar. ivaruṭiya kalveṭṭukkaḷ citamparam kōyilil 12- uḷḷaṉa. ivaṟṟuḷ perumpālaṉa aṉṉam pālippup paṟṟiyaṉa.

srīraṅkarāyar II
ivar cakam 1503 (ki.pi. 1581) -il miḷakut taraku varumāṉaṅ koṇṭu ciṟṟampalavarkku apiṣēkam ceyyavum, cakam 1517 (ki.pi. 1595) il civakāmacuntari aippaci pūraviḻāk koṇṭaruḷavum puṟappēṭṭi ūrit tēvatāṉamākak koṭuttuḷḷār. cakam 1503 (ki.pi. 1581) - il ēḻu kirāmaṅkaḷiṉ varumāṉattik koṇṭum, miḷakut tarakik koṇṭum perumāḷukku apiṣēka ārātaṉi viḻāc celavukaḷ naṭattivara ēṟpāṭu ceytuḷḷār.
srīraṅkarāyar VI
ivar cakam 1565il tillit tiruccittirak kūṭattil kōvintarācap perumāḷ muṉ maṇṭapattip paḻutu pārttār. puṇṭarīkavallit tāyār, cūṭik koṭutta nācciyār vimāṉaṅkaḷum paḻutu pārkkap peṟṟaṉa. ivar kālattil ārūr, karuṅkuḻi, kuriyāmaṅkalam, maturāntaka nallūr, uṭiyūr mutaliya kirāmaṅkaḷukkut tīrvi taḷḷupaṭi ceyyappaṭṭatu. itu nikaḻnta kālam 2.2.1644 eṉṟu el.ṭi. cāmik kaṇṇuppiḷḷiyavarkaḷ kaṇakkiṭṭuḷḷār.
vīrapūpatirāyar :
ivar niruttanātaṉ (kūttappirāṉ) tirumuṉ viḷakkukkāka taṉatu araciyal piratāṉi ceṇṭapparācā ātitta rājāvik koṇṭu 64 pacukkaḷ aḷittuḷḷār. ivar kālam pukkā II kālamāka irukkalām eṉṟu epikirāpikā (1909 pak 115) āṇṭaṟikki kūṟum.
6. nāyakka maṉṉarkaḷ :
nākamanāyakkar :
maturiyil nāyakkar āṭciyi niṟuviya vicuvanāta nāyakkar tanti nākama nāyakkar citamparattil oru carakkaṟiyi vilikku vāṅkik kōyilukku aḷittuḷḷār.
muttukkiruṣṇappa nāyakkar :
ivar (citamparattil kōvintarācap perumāḷi mīḷavum piratiṭṭi ceyta) viyappa kiruṣṇappa koṇṭappa nāyakkar makaṉāvār. ivar cakam 1520 (ki.pi. 1598)-il āṭip patiṉeṭṭām perukkiṉ pōtu koḷḷiṭattil kūttap pirāṉ tīrttaṅ koṭuttaruḷutaṟkākak koḷḷiṭattiṉ vaṭakariyil tīrtta maṇṭapam kaṭṭiyuḷḷār.
7. marāṭṭiya maṉṉar
cāmpōji :
civājiyiṉ mutal mintarākiya ivar ceñciyit talinakarākak koṇṭu koḷḷiṭattiṉ vaṭapakutiyi āṇṭavar. ēṟakkuṟiya 38 āṇṭukaḷ kuṭumiyāmaliyilum, maturiyilum pātukāppāka vikkap peṟṟirunta naṭarācap perumāṉuṭiya tiruvuruvam ivar kālattil tillic ciṟṟampalattiṟkuk koṇṭu varappeṟṟatu. ki.pi. 1684-il cepput takaṭu vēyap peṟṟuk kuṭamuḻukku nikaḻttap peṟṟatu. tillip peruṅkōyil tiruppaṇiyi ivaruṭiya atikāri `kōpālatātāji eṉpavar kaṇkāṇittu niṟivēṟṟiṉār. tillic ciṟṟampalat tavamuṉivar eṉpār tillik kōyilil naṭarācap perumāṉ mīṇṭum eḻuntaruḷa utavi purintār. aracaruṭiya kulakuruvākiya muttiyā tīṭcatar eṉpavar kumpāpiṣēkatti muṉ niṉṟu naṭatti vittār. ivar ceyta tiruppaṇikaḷ tamiḻp palkalikkaḻakattāl veḷiyiṭappeṟṟa `tañci marāṭṭiyar ceppēṭukaḷ 50 eṉṉum nūlil 45 mutal 48 vari eṇṇiṭap peṟṟa tiruvārūrc ceppēṭukaḷil eṭutturikkap peṟṟaṉa.
mukamatiyar paṭiyeṭuppuk kālattil hitar ali ik kōyilip pācaṟiyākak koṇṭamiyālum, ki.pi. 1749-il paṭittalivaṉ kōp eṉpavaṉ tēvikōṭṭikkup puṟaṅkāṭṭi ōṭivarumpōtu tillik kōyilit taṉakku araṇākak koṇṭamiyālum, ki.pi. 1753-il pirañcukkārar puvaṉakiriyik kippaṟṟit tillik kōyili araṇākak koṇṭu āṅkilēyarip puṟaṅkāṭṭiyōṭac ceytami yālum ki.pi. 1780-il nikaḻnta micūr yuttattil hayarvuṭ eṉpavar ikkōyilip paṭi taṅkum iṭamākak koṇṭu etiriyit tākkiṉamiyālum civarkkut talimik kōyilākavuḷḷa it tillip peruṅkōyilāṉatu palvēṟu iṭipāṭukaḷiyaṭintatu. aḻakiya ciṟpaṅkaḷ aḻivuṟṟaṉa. āṅkilēyar āṭci innāṭṭil nili peṟṟa piṉṉarē ipperuṅ kōyilil amitiyāṉa muṟiyil nāṭpūcaṉiyum tiruviḻākkaḷum muṉpōlat toṭarntu nikaḻvaṉavāyiṉa.
kāñcipuram pacciyappa mutaliyār tiruppaṇi
civaneṟic celvarākiya ivar, tamiḻakattiṟ ciṟappuṭiya kōyilkaḷ cilavaṟṟukku nipantam aḷittuḷḷār. tillip patiyiṟ pala nāṭkaḷ taṅkiyiruntu naṭarācap perumāṉi vaḻipaṭṭu nāṭpūcaṉikkum tiruviḻāk kaḷukkum aṟakkaṭṭaḷi niṟuviyuḷḷār. tillip peruṅkōyiliṉ kiḻakkuk kōpurattip paḻutu pārttut tiruppaṇi ceytuḷḷār.
nakarattār tiruppaṇi
civamum tamiḻum vaḷartta taṉavaṇikac celvarkaḷākiya nakarattār cārpil tilli naṭarācap perumāṉ tirukkōyil ceṉṟa pattoṉpatām nūṟṟāṇṭil mikavum virivāṉa muṟiyil tiruppaṇi ceyyap peṟṟatu. tilliyiṉ elliyil aṇṇāmalip palkalikkaḻakam niṟuviya peruṅkoṭi vaḷḷal ceṭṭināṭṭaracar rājā car. mu. aṇṇāmalic ceṭṭiyār avarkaḷuṭiya  tantiyār cā.rāma. muttiyā ceṭṭiyāravarkaḷ tillip peruṅkōyiliṉ mutaṟ pirākārattiṉ tirumāḷikip pattiyiṉip putuppittum, tiru mūlaṭṭāṉēcuvarar tirukkōyiliyum umiyapārvati tirukkōyiliyum putuppittum, iraṇṭām pirākārattiyoṭṭi aṇiveṭṭikkāl maṇṭapaṅkaḷik kaṭṭiyum, tillip poṉṉampalatti mēlum polivuṟac ceytum ki.pi. 1891- ām āṇṭil kuṭamuḻukku viḻāvic ciṟappuṟa nikaḻt tiṉārkaḷ.
ivarkaṭkuppiṉ ivartam mintar rājācar mu. aṇṇāmalic ceṭṭiyār avarkaḷ, ikkōyiliṉ civa - viṇava vēṟupāṭu akalat tilli kōvintarācap perumāḷ kōyilit tiruppaṇi ceytu naṭarācar cannitiyilum kōvintarācar cannitiyilum muṉ maṇṭapaṅkaḷik kaṭṭi ki.pi. 1934-ām āṇṭu tillik kōvintarācap perumāḷukkuk kuṭamuḻukku viḻāc ceytārkaḷ. 
cīrkāḻi capānāyaka mutaliyār tiruppaṇi
cīrkāḻi peruniḻakkiḻārākiya ivar, tilliyiṟ kaṉaka capiyil nāḷtōṟum cantiramulīvararkkum irattiṉa capāpatikkum nikaḻntuvarum tirumañcaṉatti aṉparkaḷ iruntu taricippataṟku vacatiyākak kaṉakacapiyiṉ kiḻakkē tēkku marak kūriyil cepputtakaṭu vēynta taricaṉa maṉṟatti amittārkaḷ. ittiruppaṇiyi ivarkaṭkup piṉ citamparanāta mutaliyār avarkaḷ niṟivēṟṟiṉārkaḷ.
ikkālat tiruppaṇi
ivvirupatām nuṟṟāṇṭil āṭūr perunilakkiḻār taruma pūṣaṇam em. irattiṉacapāpatip piḷḷiyavarkaḷum, citamparam aṇikala vaṇikar tarumapūṣaṇam, ce. irattiṉacāmic ceṭṭiyār avarkaḷum cērntu rūpā mūṉṟu ilaṭcattiṟku mēl celavu ceytu tillic ciṟcapiyiṉiyum kaṉakacapiyiṉiyum tiruppaṇi ceytu 1955-ām āṇṭil kuṭamuḻukku viḻāvic ciṟappuṟa nikaḻttiṉar.
piṉṉar, āṭūr perunilakkiḻār taruma pūṣaṇam, em. irattiṉa capāpatip piḷḷiyavarkaḷāl āyirakkāl maṇṭapat tiruppaṇi toṭaṅkic ceyyap peṟṟatu. avarkaḷukkup piṉ avarkaḷ peyarāl niṟuvappaṭṭa aṟakkaṭṭaḷiyiṉ cārpil paḻutu pārttu irumpuk kampikaḷāl cuṟṟilum vaciyamikkap peṟṟut tiruppaṇi iṉitu niṟivēṟiyatu.
citamparam tiru. vi. kalyāṇarāmap piḷḷiyavarkaḷāl toṭaṅkap peṟṟa civakāmiyammi tirukkōyil tiruppaṇi avarkaṭkup piṉ tiru. eṉ.em. poṉṉampalampiḷḷi yavarkaḷ talimiyil tamiḻaka aracum ceṭṭi nāṭṭaracar rājā car muttiyāc ceṭṭiyār avarkaḷum potumakkaḷum utaviya nitiyutaviyik koṇṭu niṟivu ceyyap peṟṟatu. 1972-il kuṭamuḻukku iṉitu niṟivēṟiyatu.
citamparam nakaramaṉṟat talivarākavum pārāḷumaṉṟa uṟuppiṉārākavum caṭṭamaṉṟa uṟuppiṉarākavum iruntu kūṭṭuṟavuttuṟi mutaliya pala tuṟikaḷilum nāṭuvaḷam peṟa nalla pala paṇikaḷip purinta ār. kaṉakacapip piḷḷi avarkaḷ tam tantiyār āṭūr irattiṉacapāpatip piḷḷiyavarkaḷ virumpiya vaṇṇam tillip peruṅkōyil tiruppaṇiyil īṭupāṭuṭiyarāyiṉar. avarkaḷit talivarākavum muṉṉāḷ caṭṭamaṉṟa uṟuppiṉar tiruppaṇiccelvar. ji. vākīcam piḷḷiyavarkaḷic ceyalāḷarākavum koṇṭa citamparam capānāyakar tirumatil tiruppaṇikkuḻu tillip peruṅkōyiliṉ veḷippuṟap perumatilākiya vīrappa nāyakkar matil tiruppaṇiyi iṉitu niṟivēṟṟiyatu. ikkuḻu 1972-il citamparam capānāyakar kōyil tiruppaṇik kuḻuvāka māṟṟiyamikkappeṟṟatu. appoḻutu tamiḻaka araciṉ aṟaniliyattuṟi āṇiyarākap paṇipurinta tiru. em.kē. pāla cuppiramaṇiyam avarkaḷatu utaviyuṭaṉ tillip peruṅkōyil tiruppaṇiyic ciṟappuṟa nikaḻttutaṟku muppattintu laṭca rūpā aḷavil periya tiṭṭam (māṭarpiḷāṉ) oṉṟu variyappaṭṭatu. ataṉpaṭi tamiḻaka araciṉ naṉkoṭiyāka rūpā irupatu laṭcamum, paḻani tēvattāṉam em.e. kaṭṭaḷiyiṉ cārpil rājā car em.ē. muttiya ceṭṭiyār kuṭumpattār, ceṉṉi maruttuvar ṭākṭar irattiṉa vēl cuppiramaṇiyam ākiyōr mūlam rūpā paṉṉiraṇṭu laṭcamum āka rūpā aimpatu laṭcattiṟku mēl celaviṭappaṭṭuc citamparam tirukkōyiliṉ nāṉku kōpuraṅkaḷum tiru māḷikip pattiyum mukkuṟuṇip piḷḷiyār, kaṟpaka vināyakar, cuppiramaṇiyar mutaliya mūrtti kaḷiṉ kōyilkaḷum, cuṟṟup pirākāramum, niruttacapiyum, kērampalamum, kaṉakacapiyum, āka ellāp pakutikaḷum tiruppaṇi ceyyap peṟṟut taṉittaṉiyē kuṭamuḻukku viḻā nikaḻttappeṟṟaṉa. ittiruppaṇikaḷiṉ niṟivākat tillik kūttap perumāṉukku nikaḻum aṭcaya āṇṭu tit tiṅkaḷ 29-m nāḷ (11-2-1987) putaṉkiḻami makākumpāpiṣēkam naṭipeṟṟatu. aṇmiyil naṭipeṟṟa ittiruppaṇikaḷāl tilli naṭarācap perumāṉ tirukkōyililuḷḷa ellāc cannitikaḷum uṭpuṟamatilkaḷum tiruntiya muṟiyiṟ paḻutu pārkkap peṟṟup putiya polivuṭaṉ nikaḻtal kāṇalām.

8
tillic ciṟṟampalap peruṅkōyilil vaḷarnta kalikaḷ
kaṟṟa kalviyilum iṉiyavaṉum, kalikkelām poruḷākiyavaṉum ākiya kaṇṇutaṟ kaṭavuḷ āṭal puriyum tillip peruṅ kōyilāṉatu kaṭṭaṭakkali, oviyakkali, ciṟpakkali, āṭaṟkali, icikkali mutaliya kalikaḷ palavaṟṟiyum vaḷartta taṉic ciṟappuṭiyatākat tikaḻkiṉṟatu.
uḷḷattiṟku uvakiyaḷikkum kalikaḷuḷ āṭaṟkaliyum oṉṟu, itu iyal ici nāṭakameṉum muttamiḻōṭum toṭarpuṭiyatu. ikkaliyāṉatu akakkūttu, puṟakkūttu patiṉōr āṭalkaḷ eṉap palvēṟu pakutikaḷāka vaḷarkkap peṟṟatu. ētēṉum oru katiyiṉit taḻuvi naṭikkap peṟum kūttiṉi nāṭakameṉṟum, kati taḻuvātu pāṭṭiṉatu poruḷiṉuk kēṟpak kikāṭṭi vallapañ ceyyum cuviyum meyppāṭum poruntiya avinayak kūttiṉi nāṭṭiyameṉavum vaḻaṅkutal marapu. nāṭṭiya meṉpatu, āṭal pāṭal aḻaku eṉṉum mūvaki nalaṅkaḷiyum oruṅkē peṟṟa āṭal makaḷirāl tamatu akakkuṟippu uṭampil tōṉṟum meyppāṭukaḷāl puṟattē pulappaṭumpaṭi cuvipeṟa naṭikkap peṟuvatu. itaṉ ilakkaṇat tiṉi viritturippatu nāṭṭiya nūl. nāṭṭiyak kaliyāṉatu ellām valla kūttap perumāṉatu aintoḻil tirukkūtti aṭippaṭiyākak koṇṭu tōṉṟi vaḷarntatākum. ikkaliyāṉatu ōviyam, ciṟpam, kāviyam ākiya kalikaḷ palavaṟṟōṭum toṭarpuṭiya tākum.
tillip peruṅkōyilāṉatu mēṟkuṟitta kalikaḷ ellāvaṟṟiyum vaḷarkkum ceḻuṅkali niyamamākat tikaḻkiṉṟatu, kūttap perumāṉ uyirkaḷukku iṉpantarum ulakiṉip paṭikka vēṇṭum eṉṟa tiruvuḷḷattōṭu pēriṉpat tirukkūttiṉi āṭat toṭaṅkiṉār. avaratu uṭukkiyiliruntē olikaḷ uṇṭāyiṉa. avvolikaḷē eḻuttukkaḷukkellām piṟappiṭam. eṉavē eḻuttukkaḷi mutalākak koṇṭu iyaṅkum ellā moḻikaḷum akkūttap perumāṉatu uṭukki oliyiṉiṉṟum tōṉṟiyaṉavēyām. tillic ciṟṟampalavar eḻuntaruḷiyuḷḷa ittirukkōyilil eḻunilik kōpuraṅkaḷ nāṉkilum iṟivaṉatu tirumēṉit tōṟṟaṅkaḷiyum tēvar muṉivar mutaliyōr uruvaṅkaḷiyum eḻilpeṟa vaṭittukkāṭṭum ciṟpaṅkaḷ pala iṭam peṟṟiruttal kāṇalām.
teyvat tiṟam vāynta kūttukkaḷāka nam tamiḻakattu neṭuṅkālamākap pōṟṟappeṟum ciṟappuṭiya āṭalkaḷiṟ pala ittirukkōyiṟ ciṟpaṅkaḷil iṭam peṟṟuḷḷaṉa. civaperumāṉ āṭiya nūṟṟeṭṭuk karaṇaṅkaḷum avipaṟṟiya ilakkaṇaṅkūṟum nāṭṭiya nūliṉ culōkaṅkaḷum mēlik kōpura uḷvāyilil makaḷirāṭum muṟiyil viḷakkap peṟṟuḷḷaṉa. ivvāṟē teṟku, vaṭakku, kiḻakku ākiya kōpuraṅkaḷilum ikkaraṇaṅkaḷiṉ ceyal muṟikaḷ ciṟpa amippil veḷippaṭuttappaṭṭuḷḷami kāṇalām. iṟivaṉukkuriyaṉavākac collappaṭṭa ikkaraṇaṅkaḷ, eḻunilikkōpuram nāṉkiṉ vāyil kaḷilum irupuṟamum tōḻiyar niṟka naṭuvē talimakaḷ orutti āṭum muṟiyil amikkap peṟṟiruttal kāṇalām. iṅku naṭuniṉṟāṭum āṭal makaḷiṉ vaṭivam ellām valla iṟiviyik kuṟittateṉak koḷvārumuḷar.  ipperuṅkōyilil  nirutta capiyilum civakāmiyammi kōyiliṉ uṭpirakāramākiya tirumāḷikip pattiyiṉ kuṟaṭukaḷilum, pāṇṭiya nāyakam kōyiliṉ kuṟaṭukaḷilum amintuḷḷa ciṟpaṅkaḷ āṭaṟ kaliyiṉ palvēṟu karaṇaṅkaḷiyum palappaṭuttuvaṉavāka amin tuḷḷaṉa. civakāmiyammi tirukkōyilil āṭaṟkalik kuriya aṅkamāka aminta palvēṟu icik karuvikaḷiṉ amippum, avaṟṟi ici vāṇarkaḷ vācikkum muṟiyum ciṟpaṅkaḷāka vaṭittuk kāṭṭap peṟṟuḷḷaṉa. tōṟkaruvi, tuḷikkaruvi, kañcakkaruvi, narampukkaruvi eṉappaṭum icikkaruvikaḷiṉ uruvaṅkaḷil ippoḻutu vaḻakkil illātaṉa cilavum iṭam peṟṟiruttal kāṇalām. 18-ām nuṟṟāṇṭil makamatiyar mutaliya puṟaccamayap paṭivīrarkaḷ ikkōyilip pācaṟiyākak koṇṭu taṅkiṉamiyāl, icciṟpaṅkaḷiṟ pala citivuṟṟaṉa. vīṇi, yāḻ mutaliya narampuk karuvikaḷ viralāl mīṭṭi vācittaṟkuriyaṉa eṉpataṉip palarum aṟivar. civakāmiyammi kōyiliṟ kāṇappaṭum narampuk karuvi oṉṟu ikkālattil vaḻaṅkum mēlināṭṭu icik karuviyāṉa piṭilip pōṉṟu, villiṉāl vācittaṟkuriya karuviyāka amintuḷḷami kāṇalām. ik karuviyiṉ muḻu amippiṉiyum nām kāṇa muṭiyātapaṭi kalakakkārarkaḷāl, itu citivuṟṟuk kāṇappaṭukiṉṟatu.
tillikkōyilil kutiri pūṭṭiya tērmaṇṭapamāka nirutta capiyum, yāṉiyum, yāḷiyum iḻukkum tēr amippākap pāṇṭiya nāyakam tirukkōyilum amintuḷḷami paḻaṅkālak kaṭṭaṭak kaliyiṉ ciṟappiṉi viḷakkuvatākum. ivvirumaṇṭapaṅkaḷil uḷḷa tūṇkaḷ yāvum, muḻuvatum teyvat tiruvuruviṉit taṉṉakattē koṇṭa ciṟu ciṟu tērkaḷip peṟṟaṉavāka viḷaṅkutal akkālac ciṟpikaḷiṉ nuṇṇiya vēlippāṭṭut tiṟamikku, ōr eṭuttuk kāṭṭākum.
tillip peruṅkōyil, neṭuntūrattilēyē oḷiyuṭaṉ tikaḻum cempoṉ māḷikiyākiya poṉṉampalattiṉit taṉṉakattē koṇṭu aḻakiya ciṟpaṅkaḷ palavaṟṟi yuṭiyatāy amintiruntatu eṉpataṉiyum, ataṉ naṭuvē māṇikkak kūttaṉ āṭal purintaruḷum tillic ciṟṟam palamākiya tirumaṉṟam aḻakiya ciṟanta ōviyaṅkaḷ pala eḻutappeṟṟuk kaviṉuṟat tikaḻntatu eṉpataṉiyum ciṟpan tikaḻtaru tiṇmatiṟṟilli (tirukkōviyār 315) eṉavum, cēṇiṟpoli cempoṉ māḷikit tillic ciṟṟampalattu māṇikkak kūttaṉ (tirukkōviyār 23) eṉavum, `ciṟṟam palattu eḻutum ōviyaṅ kaṇṭaṉṉa oṇṇutalāḷ (tiruk - 384) eṉavum varum toṭarkaḷil maṇivācakap perumāṉ kuṟittup pōṟṟiyuḷḷami, avar kālattilēyē tillic ciṟṟampalam ciṟpam, ōviyam mutaliya kalikaḷukku nilikkaḷaṉākat tikaḻnta ceytiyip pulappaṭuttuvatākum.
tillit tirucciṟṟampalat tirukkōyilil vaḻipāṭṭuk kālaṅkaḷil nāḷtōṟum kuṭamuḻā muḻakkap peṟṟatu. icceytiyiṉi, `ciṟṟampalattu aṭiyēṉ kaḷitarak kār miṭaṟṟōṉ naṭamāṭak kaṇṇār muḻavam tuḷitarak kāreṉa ārttaṉa (tirukkōviyār 324) eṉavarum tirukkōviyār toṭarāl aṟiyalām. civapāta cēkaraṉ eṉap pōṟṟappeṟum irājarāja cōḻaṉāl tirumuṟi kaṇṭu eṭukkappaṭṭatum mūvar aruḷiya iyalicit tamiḻākiya ittiruppatikaṅkaḷukkut tirunīlakaṇṭa yāḻppāṇar marapil tōṉṟiya ammiyār oruvarik koṇṭu paṇṭupōl iciyamittaṟkuriya aruḷ niliyamāka amintatum ittillip peruṅ kōyilē yākum.
narampuṭi yāḻ oli muḻaviṉ nātavoli vētoli
arampiyartaṅ kīta oli aṟāttilli	      (periya. taṭuttāṭa - 9)
eṉac cēkkiḻār perumāṉ iyalici nāṭakameṉum muttamiḻ vaḷarccikkum ittillip peruṅkōyil nilikkaḷamāy aminta tiṟatti viritturittuḷḷami iṅku oppu nōkkattakuvatākum.
civakāmiyammi tirukkōyilil muṉ maṇṭapak kūriyil variyap peṟṟuḷḷa ōviyaṅkaḷ mikavum paḻami vāyntaṉa. pāṇṭiya nāyakam tirukkōyiliṉ mēṟkūriyil eḻutap peṟṟa oviyaṅkaḷum ittirukkōyiliṉ piṟaviṭaṅ kaḷil aṇmiyileḻutap peṟṟa ōviyaṅkaḷum putumip polivu peṟṟut tikaḻkiṉṟaṉa. ittirukkōyilil aminta eḻu nili kōpuraṅkaḷ nāṉkum ikkālak kaṭṭaṭap poṟiyāḷar palarum viyantu pārāṭṭum muṟiyil amin tuḷḷami kāṇalām.
tillip peruṅkōyil eḻunilik kōpuraṅkaḷ nāṉkiṉuḷḷum kiḻakkuk kōpuram, tarimaṭṭam mutal viyāḷam vari 35 aṭi uyaramum 108 aṭinīḷamum 60 aṭi akalamum uṭiya karuṅkaṟkaṭṭiṭamum, ataṟkumēl viyāḷam mutal kalacam vari 115 aṭi uyaram uṭiya eḻunili māṭaṅkaḷum āka 152 aṭi uyaramuṭiyatu. ataṉmēl 7 aṭi 6 aṅkulam uyaramuṭiya 13 ceppukkalacaṅkaḷ uḷḷaṉa. iraṇṭām kōpperuñciṅkaṉāl amikkappeṟṟa ikkōpuravāyiliṉ vaṭa puṟattu māṭattilē teṟku nōkkiya niliyil ivaṉatu uruvam iṭam peṟṟuḷḷatu. ikkōpuratti munnūṟāṇṭu kaṭku muṉ paḻutupārttut tiruppaṇi ceytavar kāñcipuram pacciyappa mutaliyārāvār.
teṟkuk kōpuram tarimaṭṭam mutal viyāḷam vari 35 aṭi uyaramum 108 aṭi nīḷamum 62 aṭi akalamum uṭiya karuṅkaṟ kaṭṭiṭamum, ataṉmēl viyāḷam mutal kalacam vari 110 aṭi uyaramuḷḷa eḻunili māṭappakutiyum āka 142 aṭi uyaramuṭiyatu. mēluḷḷa 13 ceppuk kalacaṅkaḷum 8 aṭi 3 aṅkulam  uyaramuṭiyaṉa.
mēṟkuk kōpuram tarimaṭṭam mutal viyāḷam vari 37 aṭi uyaramum 102 aṭi nīḷamum 60 aṭi akalamum uṭiya karuṅkaṟ kaṭṭiṭap pakutiyum, ataṉmēl viyāḷam mutal kalacam vari 115 aṭi uyaramuṭiya eḻunili māṭamum āka 145 aṭi uyaramuṭiyatu. itaṉ mēluḷḷa 13 ceppuk kalacaṅkaḷum 6 aṭi 9 aṅkulam uyaramuṭiyaṉa. ikkōpuratti amittavaṉ caṭiyavarmaṉ cuntarapāṇṭiyaṉ. ātalāl itu cuntara pāṇṭiyaṉ tirukkōpuram eṉak kalveṭṭukkaḷiṟ kuṟikkap peṟṟuḷḷatu. ik kōpura vāyiliṉ teṉpuṟamāṭattilē vaṭakku nōkki vaḻipaṭum niliyil ippāṇṭiyaṉatu uruvam iṭam peṟṟuḷḷami kāṇalām.
vaṭakkuk kōpuram tari maṭṭam mutal viyāḷam vari 40 aṭi uyaramum 108 aṭi nīḷamum 70 aṭi akalamum uṭiya karuṅkaṟ kaṭṭiṭap pakutiyum ataṉ mēl viyāḷam mutal kalacam vari 107 aṭi uyaramuṭiya eḻunilimāṭap pakutiyum āka 147 aṭi uyaramuṭiyatu. ataṉmēl 7 aṭi 11 aṅkula uyaram vāynta 13 ceppuk kalacaṅkaḷ uḷḷaṉa.
mēṟkuṟitta eḻunilik kōpuraṅkaḷ nāṉkiṉ vāyiṟ paṭikaḷiṉ  nilikaḷik kāṇpōr tamiḻakakkaṭṭiṭakkali vallunar mēṟkoṇṭa poṟiyiyal vaḷarccikku eṭuttuk kāṭṭāka ittillip peruṅkōyil viḷaṅkuvataṉi naṉkuṇarvar. ivvāṟu tamiḻakattiṉ kaṭṭiṭakkali, ciṟpakkali, nāṭṭiyakkali, icikkali, ōviyakkali mutaliya kalikaḷ palavaṟṟiṉ vaḷarccikkum nilikkaḷamāka aminta tillip peruṅkōyiliṉ kali nalaṅkaḷip pēṇip pātukāttal tamiḻaka aracukkum intiya aracukkum tamiḻ makkaḷukkum uriya taliyāya kaṭamiyākum.

9
tillikkōyiliṉ nirvākam
tillip peruṅkōyiliṉ nirvākamaṉittum nāṭāḷum maṉṉaṉatu nēraṭip pārviyil nikaḻntamiyāl ittalam, kalveṭṭuk kaḷil taṉiyūr perumpaṟṟap puliyūr eṉa vaḻaṅkap peṟuvatāyiṟṟu. (te.i.ka.tokuti IV eṇ 226). aracaṉuṭiya atikāri oruvarō iruvarō ittirukkōyiliṉ naṭimuṟiyi, tilliyil taṅkik kavaṉittu varuvār. kōyil paṇikaḷip purintuvarum palvēṟu kuḻuviṉarum mūlapparuṣiyārum nakaramakkaḷum ivvatikārikaḷuṭaṉ kalantu kōyilukkuriya nilam mutaliya uṭimikaḷip pātukākkum poṟuppiṉi mēṟkoḷvar. icceyti, toṇṭimāṉum tiruviyāṟuṭi yāṉum maturāntakap piramarāyaṉum āḷuṭi yār kōyilukkuc camutāyat tirumāḷikik kūṟu tilli yampalap pallavarāyaṉum cīkāriyañ ceyvārkaḷum camutāyañ ceyvārkaḷum kōyil nāyakañ ceyvārkaḷum  tirumāḷikik kūṟu ceyya tiruvāy moḻintaruḷiṉapaṭi (te.i.ka.to. IV eṇ 222) eṉavarum aracu āṇiyāl naṉku pulaṉākum. mutaṟ kōpperuñciṅkaṉ āṭciyil tillip peruṅkōyil avaṉatu nērppārviyil nirvākañ ceyyap peṟṟatākat terikiṉṟatu.
iraṇṭām kōpperuñ ciṅkaṉ kālattil uṭiyār tirucciṟṟampala muṭiyār kōyili srīmākēcuvarak kaṇkāṇi ceyvārkaḷum srī kāriyam ceyvārkaḷum kōyil nāyakañ ceyvār kaḷum tirumāḷikik kūṟu ceyvārkaḷum kaṇakkarum (te.i.ka.to. XII eṇ 149) aracaṉatu āṇippaṭi kaṇkāṇittu vantaṉar eṉat terikiṉṟatu. ikkōyiliṉ nilam mutaliyaṉa paṟṟiya cāṉṟukaḷ perumpaṟṟap puliyūr mūlaparuṣiyār  kiyeḻuttuṭaṉ tirukkiyoṭṭip paṇṭārattil oṭukkap peṟṟiruntaṉa. tillip peruṅkōyilil variyappeṟṟup paṭiyeṭukkap peṟṟuḷḷa irunūṟṟeḻupatu kalveṭṭukkaḷil ikkōyiliṉ nirvākam nāṭāḷ vēntaṉiṉ āṇippaṭi tirukkōyilil paṇi ceyyum palvēṟu kuḻuviṉarālum camutāyañ ceyvārākiya nakara makkaḷālum nirvakikkap peṟṟu vanta ceytiyē iṭam peṟṟuḷḷami maṉam koḷḷat takuvatākum. puṟaccamaya makkaḷ paṭiyeṭuppiṉāl ikkōyiṟ pūci taṭippaṭṭa niliyil kūttap perumāṉ tiruvuruvattiyum iṉṟiyamiyāta piṟa uṭimi kaḷiyum pātukākkum poṟuppil ikkōyiliṟ pūci puriyum tilli vāḻantaṇarkaḷ, tam kaṭamiyāka mēṟ koṇṭu oḻukiṉamiyālum, mika nerukkaṭiyāṉa kālattil uyirt tiyākam ceytu tirukkōyil uṭimikaḷip pātukāttamiyālum ikkōyiliṉ nirvākattip piṟkālattil tillip potuvār eṉappaṭum tilli vāḻantaṇar kavaṉittu varuvārāyiṉar.
mutalām carapōciyiṉ āṭciyil ki.pi. 1719-il ēkōci yaracarāṟ ciṟappuc ceyyappeṟṟa toṇṭi maṇṭala vēḷāḷarum cēṟikiḻār kōttirattiṉarum, niyamam eṉṟa ūriṉ talivarum ākiya rāyasrī mutaliyār muttuc cellappa mutaliyār tarumamākac civap pēraṉpar tāṇṭava mūrtti cēruvikārar eṉpār vallattut tōṭṭamum oru āḷ āyatat tīrviyum tillic citampararēcuvara cuvāmikkum tillik kōvintarāyap perumāḷukkum tāṉacācaṉam ceytu citamparam akōra paṇṭāram cārpilē ariciyappa mutaliyār kaṭṭaḷi yiṉi naṭattivarum tāṇṭavaṉ tōṭṭam caravaṇit tampirāṉukku nīrvārttuk koṭuttār eṉpatum citamparam capāpatik kaṭṭaḷiyiṉi naṭattivarum caravaṇit tampirāṉ eṉpavar it tāṉacācaṉatti ēṟṟuk koṇṭu cūriya cantirar uḷḷavariyum ikkaṭṭaḷiyiṉi naṭattuvatāka oppuk koṇṭār eṉpatum tamiḻppalkalikkaḻakam veḷiyiṭṭuḷḷa `tañci marāṭṭiya ceppēṭukaḷ - 50 eṉṟa nūlil 7-ām eṇṇuḷḷa `tiruvārūrc ceppēṭu -1 il kuṟikkap peṟṟuḷḷaṉa. (pakkam. 55 - 61).
ki.pi. 26-8-1719 il aḷikkappaṭṭa ittamiḻc ceppēṭu citamparam kōyilukku uriyatākum. tiruvārūrk kōyiliṟ pātukākkap paṭṭiruntalāl, tiruvārūr ceppēṭu - 1, eṉṟa peyaril veḷiyiṭap peṟṟuḷḷatu. icceppēṭṭiṟ kuṟikkap paṭṭuḷḷa aṟakkaṭṭaḷiyiṉi ēṟṟu naṭattum poṟup puṭiyavarākavō ikkaṭṭaḷikkuriya nirvākattiṟku uriyavarākavō tīṭcitarkaḷ icceppēṭṭiṟ kuṟikkappaṭa villi. ivvaṟak kaṭṭaḷiyiṉi ēṟṟu naṭattum nirvākap poṟuppuṭiyavarākat tāṇṭavaṉtōṭṭam capāpati kaṭṭaḷiyiṉi naṭattum caravaṇattampirāṉ eṉpavarē kuṟippiṭap peṟṟuḷḷār.
icceppēṭṭiṟ kūṟappaṭṭa ceytiyiṉik kūrntu nōkkuṅkāl akkālattil tillip peruṅkōyilukku maṉṉarkaḷālum kuṭimakkaḷālum niṟuvappaṭum kaṭṭaḷikaḷi nirvakikkum poṟuppu akōrapaṇṭāram pōṉṟa tirumaṭattiṉ talivarkaḷiṭattilum caravaṇat tampirāṉ talivar kaḷiṭattilum caravaṇit tampirāṉ pōṉṟa tuṟavikaḷiṭattilum oppaṭikkappaṭṭa teṉpatum tilli vāḻantaṇarkaḷ tirukkōyiliṉ pūcaṉi muṟikaḷākiya uḷtuṟi aluvalkaḷi maṭṭumē kavaṉittuk koḷḷum urimiyāḷarāka iruntaṉar eṉpatum naṉku pulaṉātal kāṇalām.
tillip peruṅkōyilukkeṉa vēntarkaḷum kuṟunila maṉṉarkaḷum, vaḷḷalkaḷum, tēvatāṉamākak koṭuttuḷḷa ūrkaḷum nilaṅkaḷum piṟkālattavarkaḷāṟ kavarappaṭṭaṉa. eṉavē tillip peruṅkōyilukkeṉa ippoḻutu nilavuṭimi etuvumilli. kāñcipuram pacciyappa mutaliyār pōṉṟa tamiḻ muṉṉōrkaḷ niṟuviyuḷḷa aṟakkaṭṭaḷi kaḷik koṇṭē ikkōyiliṉ nāḷvaḻipāṭum tiruviḻākkaḷum ciṟappuṟa nikaḻntu varukiṉṟaṉa.
tilli vāḻantaṇarkaḷ kūttap perumāṉukkup pūcaṉi puriyum avvaḷavil maṭṭum amintuviṭāmal ikkōyiluḷḷē nikaḻum akattoṇṭukaḷ ellāvaṟṟiyum tāmē ceyyum poṟuppiṉi mēṟkoṇṭavarkaḷ eṉpatu `aṅkaṇar kōyiluḷḷāl akampaṭit toṇṭu ceyvār eṉavarum cēkkiḻār vāymoḻiyālum kuṭantiyi yaṭuttuḷḷa irācarācēccurat tirukkōyililuḷḷa tilli vāḻantaṇarkaḷik kuṟitta tiruttoṇṭat tokiyaṭiyār ciṟpattiṉālum iṉitu pulaṉākum. tilliyampalap perumāṉit tamatu vāḻvākavum vippu nitiyākavum koṇṭu vaḻipāṭu ceytu varum nāṉmaṟiyantaṇarkaḷākiya ittilli vāḻ antaṇarkaḷi `ūṟuiṉ tamiḻāl uyarntār, eṉat tiruñāṉacampantap piḷḷiyār ciṟappittup pōṟṟiyuḷḷār. piḷḷiyār aruḷiya vaṇṇam tamiḻāl uyarnta tilli vāḻantaṇarkaḷ tillik kaṉakacapiyilē centamiḻc civat tirumuṟikaḷip paṇṇuṭaṉ ōtip pōṟṟi varuvatu tamiḻ makkaḷ ellōrum pārāṭṭutaṟkuriya tiruppaṇiyākum.
akaliṭat tuyarnta tilli yantaṇar akilamellām
pukaḻtiru maṟiyōr eṉṟum potunaṭam pōṟṟi vāḻka
					(periya tilli - 10)
eṉac cēkkiḻār perumāṉ vāḻttiya aruṇmoḻi vāḻttu tilli vāḻantaṇarkku eṉṟum uriyatākum.

10
piṟcērkki
tilli naṭarācar tirukkōyilil
 kōvintarācap perumāḷ 
iṭam peṟṟa varalāṟu


centamiḻ nūlkaḷilum vaṭamoḻi nūlkaḷilum tillip peruṅkōyil civacamayattārkkuc ciṟappurimiyuṭiya talimik kōyilākavē pōṟṟap peṟṟuḷḷatu. viyākkira pātarākiya pulikkāl muṉivar tirumūlaṭṭāṉap perumāṉi vaḻipaṭṭup pōṟṟiṉamiyāl perumpaṟṟap puliyūr eṉavum, tillivaṉamākiya ittiruttalattilē patañcali muṉivar ceyta tavattiṟku aruḷkūrntu ellām valla civaperumāṉ ñāṉa mayamāṉa ampalattilē āṉantak kūttu nikaḻttiyaruḷutalāl tillic ciṟṟampalam eṉavum vaḻaṅkap peṟuvatu ittillip peruṅkōyilākum. tiruñāṉacampantar, tirunāvukkaracar, cuntarar ākiya mūvarum aruḷiya tēvārat tiruppatikaṅkaḷum, māṇikka vācakar aruḷiya tiruvācakam tirucciṟṟampalak kōviyākiya aruḷ nūlkaḷum tirumūlar tirumantiram mutal tiruttoṇṭar purāṇam īṟākavuḷḷa ēṉiya tirumuṟikaḷum tillic ciṟṟampalattiyē civattalaṅkaḷellāvaṟṟilum mutaṉmi yuṭiyatākak koṇṭu `kōyil, eṉṉum peyarāṟ ciṟappittup pōṟṟiyuḷḷaṉa. cēracōḻa pāṇṭiyarkaḷākiya tamiḻ vēntar āṭciyilum iṭikkālattil vanta pallava maṉṉarkaḷ āṭciyilum piṟkālattil vanta vicayanakara maṉṉar nāyakka maṉṉar marāṭṭiya maṉṉar āṭciyilum tillip peruṅkōyil kūttap perumāṉukkuriya tirukkōyilākavē kalveṭṭukkaḷiṟ kuṟikkap peṟṟuḷḷatu. ittirukkōyiliṉ āṭciyurimikaḷ aṉittum civaṉaṭiyārkaḷākiya mākē vararkaḷukkum kōyilil srī kāriyamākiya akattoṇṭu purintu varum tilli vāḻantaṇar mutaliyōrkkum kōyil nāyakañ ceyvār ākiya araciyalatikārikaṭkum camutāyañ ceyvārākiya ūrp potu makkaḷukkum uriyaṉavākak kēyilil variyap peṟṟa kalveṭṭukkaḷiṟ kuṟikkap peṟ ṟuḷḷaṉa. citamparam naṭarācar kōyilil variyap peṟṟuḷḷa 270kku mēṟpaṭṭa kalveṭṭukkaḷil ikkōyil uṭiyār tirucciṟṟampalamuṭiyarākiya poṉṉam palavāṇarkkuc ciṟappurimiyuṭiya tirukkōyilākak kuṟikkap peṟṟuḷḷami kāṇalām.
ivvāṟu civa camayattārkkē ciṟappurimi vāynta citamparam naṭarācap perumāṉ tirukkōyilil kōvintarācap perumāḷ tiruvuruvam eppoḻutu piratiṣṭi ceyyappaṭṭatu eṉpatum ikkōyilil srīviṣṇarkaḷukku ettakiya urimiyuṇṭu eṉpatum varalāṟṟu muṟiyil nōkkuvatu ikkōyiliṉ amitiyāṉa naṭimuṟikku mikavum uṟutuṇi ceyvatākum.
tēvāra āciriyar mūvar kālam variyilum tillip peruṅkōyilil kōvintarācap perumāḷ canniti iṭam peṟavilli. citamparam capānāyakar tirukkōyilil kōvinta rācap perumāḷi mutaṉ mutaṟ piratiṣṭi ceytavaṉ ki.pi.726 mutal 775 vari āṭcipurinta nantivarma pallavaṉ āvāṉ. icceyti tiruccittira kūṭattip pōṟṟit tirumaṅkiyāḻ vār pāṭiyaruḷiya periya tirumoḻiyāṟ pulaṉākiṉṟatu.
pim poṉṉum muttum maṇiyuṅ koṇarntu
paṭimaṉṉavaṉ pallavarkōṉpaṇinta
cempoṉ maṇimāṭaṅkaḷ cūḻnta tillit
tiruc cittirakūṭam ceṉṟu cērmiṉkaḷē.	    (periya tirumoḻi 3-2-3)
eṉat tirumaṅkiyāḻvār pāṭip pōṟṟutalāl tillip peruṅkōyililuḷḷa kōvintarācap perumāḷ canniti nantivarma pallavaṉāl mutaṉmutal amikkap peṟṟateṉpatu naṉku pulaṉākum. iccanniti tillik kūttappirāṉ tirumuṟṟattil ciṟiya tiṇṇiyaḷavil tāṉ mutaṉ mutal niṟuvappeṟṟiruntatu eṉpatu tillit tiruccittira kūṭam eṉṟa peyarāl teḷiyappaṭum. `cittirakkūṭam teṟṟiyampalam eṉpatu tivākaram. (teṟṟi - tiṇṇi) eṉavē tiṇṇi yaḷavilaminta ciṟiya iṭattilēyē nantivarma pallavaṉ kōvintarācap perumāḷip piratiṣṭi ceytāṉ eṉṟu terikiṉṟatu.
civaṉit toḻu kulamākap peṟṟu ammutalvaṉatu ūrtiyākiya iṭapattiṉi ilacciṉiyākak keṇṭavarkaḷ pallava maṉṉarkaḷ. attakiya pallavamarapilē tōṉṟiya nantivarma pallavaṉ taṉ āṭciyiṉ muṟpakutiyil civa viṇava camayaṅkaḷiṟ cama nōkkuṭiyaṉavāka viḷaṅkiṉāṉ. ivaṉ taṉ kālattil tirumāliṉ tiruvaruḷukkuriya aruḷāciriyarākat tikaḻnta tirumaṅkiyāḻvāratu toṭarpiṉāl parama viṇavaṉāka māṟi viṭṭāṉ. mukuntaṉ tiruvaṭikaḷit tavira vēṟoṉṟiṟkum avaṉ talivaṇaṅkavilli eṉat taṇṭan tōṭṭap paṭṭayam im maṉṉaṉip paṟṟik kūṟutalāl ivaṉ viṇavaṉāka māṟiya ceyti naṉku pulaṉākum. ivaṉ parama viṇavaṉāka māṟiya piṉpē tillic ciṟṟampalap peruṅkōyil muṟṟattiṟ kōvintarācap perumāṉip piratiṣṭi ceytaṉaṉ eṉṟu terikiṟatu.
tillit tiruccittirakūṭattil piratiṣṭi ceyyap peṟṟa kōvintarācap perumāḷi muṟippaṭi pūci ceytu vantavarkaḷ tilli mūvāyirar eṉac ciṟappitturikkappaṭum tilli vāḻantaṇarkaḷēyāvar. iṅkuḷḷa perumāḷ tiruvuruvam naṭarācar kōyilic cārnta cuṟṟukkōyil teyvam (parivārateyvam) eṉṟa aḷavilēyē tilli vāḻantaṇarkaḷāl muṟippaṭi pūcaṉi ceyyap peṟṟu vantatu. ivvāṟē kāñci nakaril ēkāmparanātar tirukkōyilil pari vāra teyvamāka eḻuntaruḷiyuḷḷa perumāḷi `nilāttiṅkaḷ tuṇṭattāṉ eṉat tirumaṅki yāḻvār maṅkaḷācācaṉam ceytiruppatum iṅku eḻuntaruḷiya perumāḷip pūcaṉi ceyyum urimiyiṉi ikkōyiṟ pūci muṟiyiṉarākiya āti civar iṉṟaḷavum mēṟ koṇṭiruppatum iṅku niṉittaṟ kuriyatākum. tillik kōvintarācap perumāḷ piratiṣṭi paṟṟiyum ātiyilirunta pūci muṟi paṟṟiyum ārāycciyaṟiñar mu. irākaviyaṅkār avarkaḷ `tiruccittirakkūṭam eṉṉum ārāyccik kaṭṭuriyil piṉvarumāṟu kūṟiyuḷḷār.
pim poṉṉum muttum maṇiyuṅ koṇarntu
 paṭimaṉṉavaṉ pallavarkōṉ paṇinta
 tillit tiruccittirak kūṭam	(pe.ti.ka. 3-2-3)
eṉṟu pāṭukiṉṟār. itaṉāl pallava vēntaṉoruvaṉāl ātiyil apimāṉikkappaṭṭatu ittirumāl kōyil eṉpatu teriyavarum.
pallavaṉ malliyar kōṉpaṇinta paramēccura viṇṇakaram,
 nantipaṇiceytanakar nantiyuraviṇṇaka ram	      (pe.ti. 5-10-7)
kōccōḻaṉ cērnta kōyil tirunaṟiyūr maṇimāṭam    (6,6,6,9)
eṉap paṇṭiyaracarkaḷ tiruppaṇi ceytu vaṇaṅkiya talaṅkaḷi ivvāṟē āḻvārkaḷ pāṭiyiruttal kāṇalām.
`paṇinta kōyil mutaliyaṉa, paṇiceytu piratiṣṭitta kōyil eṉṟa poruḷil muṉpu vaḻaṅkiyavi eṉpatu mēṟkāṭṭiya āḻvārkaḷ vākkukaḷiṉiṉṟum aṟiyappaṭum.
pallavarkōṉāl paṇiceytu vaḻipaṭappaṭṭatu ittiruc cittirakkūṭam eṉṟu karututal poruntum. iṅṅaṉam paṇinta pallavaṉākat tirumaṅkiyāḻvārāṟ pukaḻappaṭṭavaṉ avar kālattē parama viṣṇavaṉāka viḷaṅkiya iraṇṭām nantivarmaṉākac collalām.
iccittirakkūṭat tirumāli muṟkālattil muṟippaṭi ārātittu vantavarkaḷ tilli mūvāyiravarēyāvar.
mūvāyiranāṉ maṟiyāḷar muṟiyāl vaṇaṅka
-tēvātitēvaṉ tikaḻkiṉṟa tillit tiruccittirakūṭam
(pe.ti. 3-2-8)
tillinakart tiruccittira kūṭantaṉṉuḷ
antaṇarkaḷ oru mūvāyirava rētta
aṇimaṇiyā caṉattirunta vammāṉ	            (kula.ti. 10.2)
eṉṟa āḻvārkaḷ vākkukkaḷāl itu teriyavarukiṉṟatu. ivaṟṟāl ciṟṟampalamāṉa civālaya vaḻipāṭṭiyum, teṟṟiyam palamāṉa cittirakkūṭa vaḻipāṭṭiyum, muṟippaṭi purintu vantavarkaḷ tilli mūvāyiravar eṉpatu viḷakkamām. (ceṉṉip palkalik kīḻttici moḻi ārāyccittuṇar tokuti III (1938-39) pakuti - I)
eṉac ciṟanta ārāycciyāḷarum srīviṣṇavarumāṉa irāvcākēp mu. irākaviyaṅkāravarkaḷ kāytaluvattalakaṟṟi naṭuniṉṟu kūṟiya āyvu muṭivukaḷ aṟiñarkaḷatu pārāṭṭukku uriyaṉavākum.
tillip peruṅkōyilil iṭikkālattil nantivarma pallavaṉāl piratiṭṭi ceyyap peṟṟa kōvintarācap perumāḷukkuc civacamayat tavarākiya tilli mūvāyiravar pūci ceytu varuvataṉik kaṇṭu maṉam poṟukkāta piṟkāla vīraviṣṇavarkaḷiṟ cilar ciṟukac ciṟukat tillit tirucciṟṟampalak kōyil naṭimuṟikaḷukkut tolliyuṇ ṭākki vantaṉar. avarkaḷāṟ ceyyappaṭṭuvarum tollikaḷ nāḷukku nāḷ atikarittaṉa. avaṟṟāl tillic ciṟṟampalat tiruppaṇikaḷum nāṭpūcaṉi kaḷum taṭipaṭuvaṉavāyiṉa. atukaṇṭu maṉam poṟāta iraṇṭāṅ kulōttuṅka cōḻaṉ ivviṇavarkaḷ ceyyum tollikaḷuk kellām oru kāraṇamākavuḷḷatu kōvintarācap perumāḷ mūrttamē yeṉa eṇṇi ataṉit tillip peruṅkōyiliṉiṉṟum appuṟap paṭuttiṉaṉ eṉpataṉi ivaṉuṭiya avikkaḷap pulavarākiya kaviccakkara vartti oṭṭakkūttar tāmpāṭiya ulāvilum takkayākapparaṇiyilum kuṟippiṭṭuḷḷār.
iraṇṭāṅ kulōttuṅkaṉ tillik kōyiliṉṟum tirumāl mūrttatti appuṟappaṭuttiya icceyalip piṟkālattil viṇavarkaḷāl eḻutappaṭṭa tivya cūricaritam, kōyiloḻuku mutalāṉa nūlkaḷ mikippaṭuttik kūṟi ivvēntaṉ mītu aṭāta paḻikaḷic cumattiyum, kirumikaṇṭa cōḻaṉ eṉa ivaṉi iḻittuk kūṟiyum uḷḷaṉa. innūlkaḷil ivvēntaṉik kuṟittuk kūṟappaṭuvaṉavellām veṟuṅ kaṟpaṉik katikaḷē; uṇmiyāvaṉa alla eṉpatu varalāṟṟāciriyarkaḷ tuṇipākum. (piṟkālac cōḻar carittiram, aṇṇāmalip palkalikkaḻaka veḷiyīṭu, pakkam. 95,96 pārkka).
iraṇṭāṅ kulōttuṅkaṉ āṭcikkālattil tillik kōyililiruntu appuṟappaṭuttappaṭṭa kōvintarācap perumāḷ tiruvuruvatti irāmānucar kīḻit tiruppatiyiṟ koṇṭu cērttu aṅkē piratiṭṭi ceytār eṉa irāmānuja tivya cariti kūṟukiṟatu. irāmānucarāl piratiṭṭi ceyyappaṭṭa tiruvuruvam iṉṟum aṅkēyuḷḷateṉpatum, ippōtu citamparam kōyililuḷḷa kōvintarācap perumāḷ tiruvuruvam  vicayanakara aracar accutarāyar kālattil putitākap piratiṭṭi ceyyap paṭṭa teṉpatum varatarāja aiyaṅkār pāṭiya pākavata purāṇattil tiruvaraṅkap paṭalattiluḷḷa 63, 99 ām ceyyuṭkaḷāl  naṉku pulaṉākum. eṉavē irāṇṭāṅ kulōttuṅka cōḻaṉ āṭcik kālamākiya ki.pi. paṉṉiraṇṭām nūṟṟāṇṭiṟkum accutarāyar āṭcik kālamākiya ki.pi. patiṉāṟām nūṟṟāṇṭiṟkum iṭippaṭṭa kālattilē citamparam naṭarācap perumāṉ kōyilil kēvintarācap perumāḷ mūrttam illiyeṉṟu terikiṟatu. ki.pi. 1251 mutal  1271 vari āṭci purintuvarum civa viṇava camayaṅkaḷ iraṇṭiṉiyum oppa matittup pōṟṟiyavarum, `āmuttamāvyatā eṉṉum nūli iyaṟṟi nācciyārākiya āṇṭāḷip pōṟṟiya varum srī viṣṇavarum, ākiya vicayanakara maṉṉar kiruṣṇatēvarāyar ki.pi. 1516-il poṭṭaṉūril tām peṟṟa veṟṟikku aṭiyāḷamākat tilli naṭarācap perumāṉ tirukkōyiliṉ vaṭakkuk kōpurattik kaṭṭi muṭittup poṉṉampala vāṇarkku nilam vaḻaṅkiyuḷḷār (te.i.ka.tokuti VII eṇ 199). itaṉiyuṟṟu nōkkuṅkāl kiruṣṇa tēvarāyar kālattil kōvintarācap perumāḷ cannitiyilliyeṉpatum, tillip peruṅkōyil poṉṉampala nātarākiya civaperumāṉukkē ciṟappuṭiya tirukkōyil eṉṉum karuttuṭiyavar kiruṣṇa tēvarāyar eṉpatum naṉku viḷaṅkum.
kiruṣṇa tēvarāyarukkup piṉ ki.pi. 1529-il paṭṭattukku vanta accutatēvarāyar ki.pi. 1539-il tilli naṭarācar kōyilil kōvintarācap perumāḷi mīṇṭum piratiṣṭi ceytu vikānaca cūttirattiṉpaṭi pūci naṭakka 500 poṉ varuvāyuḷḷa nāṉku kirāmaṅkaḷiṉ variyi nīkkik koṭuttuḷḷār. ivar perumāḷi mīṇṭum piratiṭṭi ceyta iṭam muṟkālattil nantivarma pallavaṉ piratiṭṭi ceytirunta tiṇṇiyaḷavākiya ciṟiya iṭamē eṉpatu iṅku kavaṉittaṟkuriyatākum. āyiṉum iṅku eḻuntaruḷuvitta perumāḷip pūcikka srī viṣṇavarkaḷi niyamittamiyāl perumāḷi vaḻipāṭu ceyyum urimiyi aracaṉ vaḻiyākap peṟṟa avviṇavarkaḷ mella mellat taṅkaḷukkuriyaṉavāka naṭarācar kōyiliṭaṅkaḷi valitiṟ kippaṟṟik koḷḷa muyaṉṟu vantārkaḷ. vēṅkaṭapati tēvama kārāyar āṭciyil avaruṭiya piratinitiyākac ceñciyiliruntu āṭci purinta viyappa kiruṣṇappa koṇṭama nāyakkaṉ eṉṉum atikāri viṇavar muyaṟcikku uṭantiyāyk ki.pi. 1597-il naṭarācar kōyil mutaṟ pirākārattilēyē kōvintarācap perumāḷukkut taṉik kōyili amikkat toṭaṅkiṉāṉ. inniliyil tillip peruṅkōyiliṉ pūciyurimiyit toṉṟutoṭṭup peṟṟuṭiyarākiya tilli vāḻantaṇarkaḷum nakarap potumakkaḷum naṭarācap perumāṉukkēyuriya ikkōyilil perumāḷukkeṉap putitākat taṉikkōyil kaṭṭutal vēṇṭām eṉavum iraṇṭām pirakārattil muṉṉirunta iṭattilēyē perumāḷukkuc pūci nikaḻac ceytalē poruttamākum eṉavum avvatikāriyi evvaḷavō muṟi nayantu kēṭṭārkaḷ. koṇṭatu viṭāk koṇṭama nāyakkaṉākiya avvatikāri avarkaḷatu vēṇṭu kōḷip poruṭpaṭuttāmal kōvintarācar cannitiyip poṉṉam palavāṇar cannitikku mikavum arukilēyē amikkat toṭaṅkiṉāṉ. anniliyil tilli vāḻantaṇar kaḷ taṅkaḷ uyirik koṭuttēṉum av vaṉceyalit taṭuttu niṟutta uṟuti pūṇṭaṉar; tāṅkaḷ uyirōṭirukkum vari naṭarācar kōyiliṉ uṭpuṟattil kōvintarācap perumāḷ kōyil kaṭṭutaṟku iṇaṅka māṭṭōm eṉṟu colli oruvar piṉ oruvarākak kōpurattiṉ mēlēṟik kīḻē vīḻntu uyir viṭuvārāyiṉar. ivvāṟu irupatu pērvari taṟkoli ceytu koṇṭa tuṉpakkāṭciyit taṉ kaṇkaḷāṟ kaṇṭum maṉamiraṅkāta koṇṭama nāyakkaṉ kōpurattiṉ mēlēṟit taṟkoli ceyya muntupavarkaḷiyellām cuṭṭut taḷḷumpaṭi uttaraviṭṭāṉ. ataṉpaṭi tilli vāḻantaṇar iruvar cuṭappaṭṭu iṟantaṉar. ittuṉpa niliyip poṟukkalāṟṟātu tilli vāḻantaṇar kuṭumpattic cērnta ammiyār oruvar tamatu kaḻuttiyaṟuttut taṟkoli ceytu koṇṭār.
ivvāṟu aracāṅka atikāriyoruvaṉatu camayap piṭivātaṅ kāraṇamākac citamparam kōyilukkuḷḷē ittuṉpac ceyalkaḷ nikaḻnta nāḷil mēli nāṭṭiliruntu yāttiri kāraṇamākat tamiḻ nāṭṭiṟkuc cuṟṟuppayaṇañ ceyta pimeṇṭā (Jesevit Father N. Pimenta) eṉṉum pātiriyār koṇṭama nāyakkaṉ ceyta ikkoṭuṅkōṉmiyi nēriṟ kaṇṭu varuntiyatuṭaṉ ik koṭuñceyalit tammuṭiya yāttirik kuṟippilum kuṟittuḷḷār. innikaḻcciyiyuṇarum nallaṟivuṭiyōr evarum kaṇṇīr viṭṭu varuntuvar eṉpatu tiṇṇam.
The Aravidu Dynasty by Father Heras (P. 553)
A notable instance of the struggle between the two sects is the lamentable event that took place at Chidambaram in 1597 A.D. While Krishnappa Nayaka of Jiniji himself a Stunch Vaishnava, was there Superintending the improvements which he had ordered at the temple of Govind Raja with in the great Saiva Temple. Father N. Pimenta, who passed through chidambaram at this time narrates in one of his letters that on this occasion a great controversy arose as to Whether it were lawful to place the signe of Perumal in the temple at Chidambaram. Some refused, others by their legates importunatly urged and the Naiks of Gingee decreed to erect in the temple `These last words of pimenta indicate that after the retoration of the idol to the temple by Rama Rayan it had agin been removed and its shrine probably destroyed. In order to re-instal if with due honour, Krishnappa Nayaka ordered the old shrine to be repired and even perhaps enlarged.’
This was the cuse of the whole trouble. “The priests of the Temple which were the Treasuries” Continues, pimenta(were) with standing and threatening if it were done, to cast down themselves from the top. The Brahmanes of the temple swore to do the like after they buried the former which yet after better advice they performed not. “But Krishnappa Nayaka was unmoved by any such threat. When the reconstruction of the shrine was carried on without hesitation where upon the priests climbing one of the high Gopurams of the temple started to cast themselves down while the Nayak was in the temple. “About twenty had perished in that precipitation on that day of our departure, whereat the Naichus angry, cused his gunners to shoot at the rest which killed two of them. A woman also was so hot in this Zealous quarrel that she cut her own throat.” Natrually Krishnappa Nayaka accomplished his purpose in spite of this opposition.
nantivarma pallavaṉ tirumāl mūrttattit tillik kōyilil piratiṣṭi ceyta kālattil attirumēṉi tiṇṇiyaḷavāka amitta ciṟiya iṭattilētāṉ vaḻipaṭap peṟṟatu. piṉ nāṉūṟu āṇṭukaḷ kaḻittu accutarāyar piratiṣṭi ceytatum atē iṭattil tāṉ. camaya veṟipiṭitta viyappa kiruṣṇappa koṇṭama nāyakkaṉ eṉpāṉ ki.pi. 1597-il muṉṉirunta atteṟṟiyam palattaḷavil nillātu naṭarācar canniti mukappiluḷḷa iṭattiyum cērttuk koṇṭu kōvintarācap perumāḷukkut taṉikkōyil kaṭṭiviṭṭāṉ. inta vampukaḷukkellām kāraṇam viṣṇavatti eṅkum parappa vēṇṭumeṉṟu muyaṉṟa vicayanakara aracarkaḷum avarkaḷukkut tuṇi niṉṟa avarkaḷuṭiya kuru tātācāriyārum āvar eṉṟu aṇṇāmalip palkalik kaḻakattil varalāṟṟuttuṟip pērāciriyarāka iruntu kālañceṉṟa ci.e. cīṉivācāccāriyār avarkaḷ maṉam varunti eḻutiyuḷḷārkaḷ.
kiruṣṇappa nāyakkaṉukkup piṉ vicayanakara maṉṉaṉākiya cīraṅkarāyaṉ -III ki.pi. 1643-il tillik kōvintarācar cannitiyi mēlum virivupaṭutta eṇṇi muṉpu illāta puṇṭarīkavallit tāyār canniti mutaliya putiya cannitikaḷiyum tillik kōyilil amittāṉ. ivarkaḷuṭiya mataveṟi kāraṇamākat tilli naṭarācar kōyiliṟ paḻamiyāka irunta civacannitikaḷ iṭikkappaṭṭu maṟintupōyiṉa. ivvāṟu viṣṇavarkaḷ koñcaṅ koñcamākat tillikkōyiliṉ perum pakutiyit tamakku urimiyākkikkoḷḷum muyaṟciyil īṭupaṭuvārāyiṉar. ataṉiyuṇarnta tillinakarac civap perumakkaḷum tilli vāḻantaṇarkaḷum iṉi iṭamilli yeṉṟu kūṟum aḷavukkup perumāḷ cannitiyiyē appuṟap paṭuttum muyaṟciyil īṭupaṭuvārāyiṉar. anniliyil kōvintarācap perumāḷ pūci muṟiyiṉik kaṇkāṇikkum urimiyuṭiya srī viṣṇavarkaḷ ki.pi. 1862-il tillip peruṅkōyil pūci muṟi urimiyāḷarākiya tilli vāḻantaṇarkaḷōṭu ōr uṭaṉpaṭikki ceytu koḷḷa vēṇṭiya iṉṟiyamiyāmi  uṇṭāyiṟṟu. citamparam kōvintarācap perumāḷ pūci muṟiyik kavaṉikkum urimiyuṭiya srī viṣṇavarkaḷ tilli vāḻantaṇarkaṭku eḻutik koṭutta uṭaṉpaṭukkiyil tāṅkaḷ kōvintarācap perumāḷukkuc ceytu varum nittiya pūcikaḷit tavira vēṟu piramōṟcavam naṭattuva tilliyeṉavum, tilliyil naṭarācap perumāṉukkut toṉṟu toṭṭu naṭipeṟṟuvarum nittiya pūcikaḷilum tiruviḻākkaḷilum tām taṭiyāka iruppatilli yeṉavum oppukkoṇṭu uṟuti kūṟiyuḷḷārkaḷ. ivvuṟutiyiṉ pēril ki.pi. 1867-il nītimaṉṟam tīrppaḷit tuḷḷatu. tillip peruṅkōyilil civar viṇavar ākiya irutiṟattārkkum ēṟpaṭṭa uṭaṉpāṭum tilli vāḻantaṇarkaḷ peṟṟuḷḷa nītimaṉṟat  tīrppum kāraṇamākat tillip peruṅkōyilil civarum viṇavarkaḷum aṉpiṉāl oṉṟukūṭi naṭarācar kōvintarācar ākiya iruperun teyvaṅkaḷiyum vaḻipaṭṭu makiḻtaṟkuriya amitiyāṉa cūḻnili toṭarntu nilipeṟṟuḷḷatu eṉpataṉi ittirukkōyilukku varum aṉparkaḷ ellōrum naṉkuṇarvar. ittakiya amiti niliyē eṉṟum niṉṟu nilavi iṉpam aḷippatākuka.
2. tilliyil āṭalpuriyum kūttapperumāṉ tiruvuruvam iṭampeyarntu mīṇṭum eḻuntaruḷiṉami
tillic ciṟṟampalattil civakāmiyammi kāṇat tirunaṭam purintaruḷum kūttap perumāṉatu tiruvuruvam ki.pi. patiṉēḻām nūṟṟāṇṭil mukamatiyar paṭiyeṭuppiṉāl citamparattiṉiṉṟum iṭampeyarntu cellavēṇṭiya iṉṟiyamiyāmi ēṟpaṭṭatu. puṟaccamayattavarāl naṭarācap perumāṉ tiruvuru citikkap peṟṟu viṭum niliyēṟpaṭumō eṉa añciya tilli vāḻantaṇarkaḷ, ammūrttiyi oru pēḻiyil vittu ayalavar aṟiyātavāṟu citamparattiṉiṉṟum eṭuttuc ceṉṟaṉar. etirppaṭṭa ciṟṟūroṉṟiṉ puṟattē makkaḷ naṭamāṭṭamillāta puḷiyantōppiṉi naḷḷaravil aṭintaṉar. attōppiluḷḷa periya puḷiyamaram oṉṟu periya pontiṉi yuṭiyatākat teṉpaṭṭatu. apponti ṉuḷḷē naṭarāca mūrttiyi oruvarum aṟiyātapaṭi pātukāppāka vittu mūṭiviṭṭut tirumpiṉar. cilanāḷ kaḻittu appuḷiyantōppukkuc contakkārarākiya vēḷāḷar oruvar tamakkuc contamāṉa puḷiyamarattiṉ perumpontu aṭikkap paṭṭiruttal kaṇṭu ataṉi veṭṭip pārttār. ataṉkaṇ naṭarācar tiruvuruvam maṟittu vikkap peṟṟiruttalik kaṇṭār. avvāṟu maṟikkap paṭṭa cūḻniliyiyuṇarntu oruvarum aṟiyātavāṟu appontiṉi naṉṟāka mūṭiviṭṭār. ammarattil teyvamiruttalit tām kaṉavu kaṇṭatāka ūr makkaḷukkuc colli ammarattiṟkup pūci ceytu vantār.
puṟac camayattār paṭiyeṭuppiṉāl nērnta acca nili nīṅkiyapiṉ tilli vāḻantaṇarkaḷ tām pontil putittu vitta naṭarāca mūrttiyi eṭuttu vantu mīṇṭum tillip poṉṉampalattil vaḻipāṭu ceyya eṇṇit tām naṭarāca mūrttiyip pātukāppāka vitta ciṟṟūriyaṭintaṉar. āṇṭukaḷ pala ceṉṟamiyālum naḷḷiravil vittamiyālum ammūrttiyip putittu vitta iṭam iṉṉateṉa aṟintu koḷḷa iyalāmal maṉakkalakkamuṭiyavarāy niṉṟaṉar, puḷiyan tōppiṉarukē māṭu mēyttuk koṇṭirunta mutiyavaroruvar ucci vēḷiyil taṉṉuṭaṉirunta ciṟuvaṉi nōkki `tampi, anta ampalappuḷiyilē koṇṭu māṭukaḷi viṭu. nāṉvantu viṭuvēṉ eṉak kūṟiṉār. ataṉik kēṭṭa tilli vāḻantaṇarkaḷ ammarattiṟku `ampalappuḷi eṉṟa peyarvarak kāraṇam eṉṉa eṉa viṉaviṉar. `eṉakkut teriyātu; eṅkaḷ ecamāṉ immarattiṟku aṭikkaṭi pūci pōṭuvār eṉṟār kiḻavar. ataṉik kēḷviyuṟṟa antaṇarkaḷ marattukkuc contakkārar yār? eṉak kēṭṭut terintu avariyaṭintu atupaṟṟik kēṭṭaṉar. avarum tamakkuriya puḷiyamarap pontil naṭarācap perumāṉ maṟittu vikkap peṟṟiruttali oruvarum aṟiyātapaṭi pātukāttu varuvataṉi eṭutturittār, tillivāḻantaṇarum avaratu icivu peṟṟu naṭarāca mūrttiyip poṉṉampalattil eḻuntaruḷac ceytu muṉpōl pūcaṉi ceyvārāyiṉar. naṭarācar maṟikkap peṟṟirunta ampalappuḷiyit taṉṉakattē koṇṭuḷḷa ciṟṟūr aṉṟu mutal puḷiyaṅkuṭiyeṉa vaḻaṅkap peṟṟatu. naṭarācar tiruvuruviṉip pātukāttuvanta avvēḷāḷar kuṭumpattār puḷiyaṅkuṭiyār eṉa aḻikkap peṟṟaṉar. cevi vaḻiyāka vaḻaṅkap peṟṟuvarum icceyti,
teḷivan tayaṉmā vaṟiyāta tillip patiyampalavāṇar
puḷiyam pontiṉiṭam vāḻum putumi kāṭṭip poruḷkāṭṭi
eḷitiṟ puḷiyaṅ kuṭiyāreṉ ṟicikkumperumi ēruḻavar
vaḷaruṅ kuṭiyiṟ polivāḻvu vaḷañcēr cōḻa maṇṭalamē   (99)
eṉac cōḻa maṇṭalac catakac ceyyuḷil iṭam peṟṟuḷḷami kāṇalām. ippāṭaliyūṉṟi nōkkuṅkāl naṭarācar tiruvuruvam maṟittu vikkappeṟṟa puḷiyaṅkuṭi eṉṉum ūr cōḻa maṇṭalattic cārnta ciṟṟūr eṉṟē karuta vēṇṭiyuḷḷatu. makāma kōpāttiyāya ṭākṭar u.vē.cāminātiyar avarkaḷ eḻutiya `ampalappuḷi eṉṉum kaṭṭuri yil icceyti veḷiyiṭap peṟṟuḷḷatu. (niṉivu mañcari - iraṇṭām pākam - pakkam 1-10)
